embedding dimension 是 2048。这是一个很苛刻的压缩比，正适合检验 projector 是否 stale。结果上，Ori 将现有低秩基线相对 full-rank Adam 的性能差距最多缩小 69.3%。摘要和正文都把这个数字作为主结果，说明收益主要来自低 rank 区间，而不是宽松设置下的小幅改进。Figure 1 还给出 performance–memory 图，Ori 基本落在左下前沿，表示在较低内存和较低计算下取得更好的精度。\n\n微调部分，作者在 RoBERTa-Base 上做 16 个场景比较，rank 更低，只有 8 或 4。Ori 在其中 10/16 个场景优于对比方法。这个结果说明方法不只适用于大规模预训练，也能迁移到更低秩、更小模型的下游优化设置，但优势没有预训练那么整齐，符合预期：微调任务噪声更大，任务间差异也更强。\n\n消融实验很有信息量。第一，增大 projector update period 会稳定地拉低性能，作者把它解释为 staleness cost 的量化证据。这个消融直接支持全文主张：问题不在于有没有低秩投影，而在于投影是否跟得上漂移。第二，引入小幅 carry-over，即 ρ≈0.99 的漂移连续性保留，会提升精度，说明 moment transfer 不是附属技巧，而是 Ori 成功的重要组成部分。第三，文中还展示了梯度子空间漂移的经验测量，证明 warm-up 后相邻步或短窗口内的主子空间距离较小，这为 streaming tracking 的前提提供了数据支撑。\n\n如果从预训练工程角度解读，最值得注意的不是单个 benchmark 数字，而是三条经验规律：子空间漂移是慢的；stale projector 的代价在低 rank 下很高；保留优化器统计连续性比每次重置更稳。这三点都很贴近真实训练系统的痛点。","reading_guide":"这篇值得精读。建议按“现象—算法—消融—理论”的顺序看。\n\n先看 Introduction 和 Ori Optimizer 开头，重点抓作者如何证明 gradient subspace drift 是 slow and stable，而不是随机跳动。这里决定了整篇方法是否站得住。然后看算法部分，尤其是 modified Oja++ / streaming k-PCA 更新、projector 如何高频更新、以及 Adam 一阶二阶矩如何在相邻基之间转移。读这部分时要一直问两个问题：为什么不继续用周期 SVD？为什么 moment transfer 合理而不是引入偏差？\n\n实验部分优先看 LLaMA-1B 预训练主表和 performance–memory 图，再看 update period 与 ρ 的消融。这里能最清楚地看到 Ori 的收益来自哪里。若时间有限，理论部分可以后读；但如果你在做 optimizer 研究，dynamic regret 那节值得看，因为它把“慢漂移可跟踪”写成了可分析对象。\n\n建议重点关注的图表通常包括：子空间漂移测量图、性能-内存前沿图、不同 rank 下与 GaLore/OSD/LoRA/Adam 的对比表、update period 消融、drift coefficient 消融。阅读时间大约 60–90 分钟。深挖点有三个：一是 Oja++ 更新在噪声梯度下的稳定性边界；二是 moment transfer 是否会在长训练中积累失配；三是这种 slow-drift 假设在更大模型、更长 token budget、MoE 或 curriculum 变化下是否仍成立。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the central weakness of low-rank gradient-subspace optimization for LLM training: the principal gradient subspace is not static. Methods in the GaLore family rely on the observation that gradients are largely concentrated in a low-dimensional subspace, but they usually refresh that subspace only periodically, often via expensive SVD recomputation, implicitly assuming it stays valid for a while. Other methods, such as OSD, add a secondary optimization loop to chase the moving subspace. The paper argues, with explicit empirical evidence, that after warm-up the gradient subspace in LLM training typically drifts slowly and smoothly rather than jumping erratically. If that is true, then the right problem is not repeated cold-start estimation but online tracking. Ori is designed to solve exactly that: how to track a slowly drifting gradient subspace cheaply and stably under very tight rank and memory budgets, and turn better tracking into better end-to-end training.","importance":"$d","technical_approach":"$e","experiments":"$f","reading_guide":"$10","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻看方法和 ablation；若“梯度子空间慢漂移”成立，低秩预训练优化器的更新频率 prior 要改。","en":"Read the method and ablations; if slow gradient-subspace drift holds, the update-frequency prior for low-rank pretraining optimizers needs revision."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xinyu Luo","Jincheng Zhou","Mengbo Wang","Zilin Shen","Nadia Atallah Lanman","Petros Drineas","Bruno Ribeiro","Brian Bullins"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["low-rank-optimization","LLM-pretraining","streaming-PCA","GaLore","optimizer"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22794","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.22794","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.23571","title":"BMuon: Budgeting Muon's Spectral Geometry for Structured Compressibility","abstract":"Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for neural network training and admits an interpretation as steepest descent under a spectral-norm geometry. Yet we find that Muon-trained Transformers can degrade sharply when their MLP hidden channels are pruned after training. To reconcile Muon's optimization performance with structured compressibility, we introduce Budget Muon (BMuon), which modifies Muon's spectral geometry so that hidden channels across blocks compete for a shared allocation budget. T","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T14:52:56Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23571.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决 Muon 优化器和结构化可压缩性之间的冲突。Muon 训练效果强，但用它训练出的 Transformer MLP 通道在训练后做全局结构化剪枝时会很脆弱。BMuon 的目标是在保留 Muon 谱范数几何优势的同时，让 MLP hidden channels 在训练期间形成可剪枝的全局分配结构。","importance":"这对预训练研究有直接意义。大模型的 MLP 占参数和 FLOPs 的大头，训练阶段是否形成结构化冗余，会影响后续压缩成本和服务效率。常见做法是在训练后加剪枝、门控或恢复微调，但这会带来额外算力，也可能破坏已训练模型。BMuon 把“通道预算”放进优化器几何，而不是放进 forward gate 或显式权重约束，提供了一条训练时诱导可压缩性的路线。贡献量级主要在优化器和训练动态层面：它不是单纯剪枝算法，而是修改 Muon 的 steepest-descent oracle，使优化方向本身带有全局通道竞争。","technical_approach":"Muon 可以理解为谱范数几何下的最速下降：每个矩阵更新来自谱范数球上的线性最小化 oracle。BMuon 改的是这个 oracle。对于第 ell 个 MLP block 的第 j 个 hidden channel，它把 up-projection 的对应行和 down-projection 的对应列作为一个耦合通道组，并分配一个 allocation μ_j^ell ∈ [0,1]。所有 block、所有 hidden channels 共享一个全局预算 K，满足 sum_{ell,j} μ_j^ell = K。给定 allocation 后，BMuon 在 Muon 的谱范数约束内部加入对角重加权；然后再全局选择 allocation，使一阶 loss decrease 最大。这样，通道之间不是各层独立竞争，而是在全模型 MLP 通道池中竞争同一个预算。理论上，BMuon oracle 等价于某个诱导范数 Ω_{α,K} 下的归一化最速下降。α 控制通道重加权强度，K 控制可用通道预算。α=0 或 K=N 时退化为 Muon；α=1 时，单位球是至多 K 个耦合通道上 operator-norm-bounded updates 的凸包；α=K=1 时接近 group-lasso 式的 l1 group 聚合。它和 NuMuon 的区别也比较清楚：NuMuon 用 nuclear-norm 约束改善低秩可压缩性，BMuon 用全局 hidden-channel budget 改善结构化通道可剪枝性。为了避免每步精确求解 allocation，算法使用 minorization–maximization surrogate 近似更新 allocation，然后执行对应的 weighted Muon update。","experiments":"给出的关键数字很有辨识度。在 ImageNet-1K 上训练 ViT-S/16，普通 Muon 训练后未剪枝 Top-1 为 75.23%，但做 30% 全局 MLP channel pruning 后掉到 3.17%，说明 Muon 的谱几何没有自然带来通道级结构冗余。BMuon 的未剪枝精度为 74.17%，在无 post-pruning recovery 的情况下剪掉 70% MLP channels 后仍有 74.08% Top-1，剪枝前后几乎不掉点。全文片段还说明它在 CIFAR-100 和 ImageNet-1K 上，在 gradual pruning 和 post-training pruning 两种设置下都优于 Muon。更值得关注的消融方向包括 α 和 K 如何控制精度-可压缩性权衡、全局预算相比 layerwise budget 是否更稳定、以及 allocation 分布是否能解释不同层的通道保留比例。","reading_guide":"建议精读，约 1.5–2 小时。先看 Introduction 中 Figure 1 对 Muon 和 BMuon 的剪枝曲线对比，这是问题动机。然后读方法部分中 allocation μ、全局预算 K、加权谱范数约束和 oracle 的定义，关键是理解它不是在 forward 中加 gate，而是在 update geometry 中改变最速下降方向。理论部分重点看 Ω_{α,K} 的定义、α=0/K=N/α=1 等边界情形，以及 representation cost 定理；这些内容解释了为什么它会偏向 width-minimal factorization。实验部分重点看三类图表：剪枝比例-精度曲线、α/K 消融、以及不同层的通道 allocation 分布。如果有大语言模型实验，需要优先检查它是否只在 ViT 上成立，还是对 Transformer LM 的 MLP 同样有效。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a mismatch between Muon’s optimization geometry and structured compressibility. Muon trains Transformers well, but the resulting MLP hidden channels can be extremely fragile under global channel pruning. BMuon aims to preserve Muon’s spectral-norm training behavior while making MLP channels compete for a global allocation budget during training.","importance":"This is directly relevant to pretraining and large-model optimization. MLP blocks account for a large fraction of Transformer parameters and inference compute, so whether training induces structured redundancy matters for compression and serving. Instead of adding post-hoc pruning, forward gates, or recovery fine-tuning, BMuon changes the optimizer’s update geometry. The contribution is best viewed as compression-aware optimization: the update direction itself carries a global channel-allocation bias.","technical_approach":"$11","experiments":"The main reported result is sharp. On ImageNet-1K with ViT-S/16, a Muon-trained model reaches 75.23% Top-1 before pruning, but drops to 3.17% after only 30% global MLP channel pruning. BMuon reaches 74.17% before pruning and keeps 74.08% Top-1 after 70% MLP channel pruning, without post-pruning recovery. The provided text also states that BMuon outperforms Muon on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1K under both gradual pruning and post-training pruning. The most informative ablations to inspect are the effects of α and K, global versus layerwise allocation behavior, and the learned allocation distribution across Transformer blocks.","reading_guide":"Read closely, in about 1.5–2 hours. Start with the pruning-collapse example in Figure 1, since it defines the empirical failure mode of Muon. Then focus on the method section: the coupled channel group, the global budget K, the diagonal reweighting inside the spectral constraint, and the resulting oracle. The key point is that BMuon does not add forward gates; it changes the geometry of the optimizer update. In the theory section, read the definition of Ω_{α,K}, the limiting cases that recover Muon or group-sparse behavior, and the representation-cost result explaining the preference for width-minimal factorizations. In the experiments, prioritize pruning-accuracy curves, α/K ablations, and per-layer allocation plots. The memory bottleneck of standard end-to-end backpropagation lies in the need to store all network activations until the loss gradient has been propagated back through. Local learning avoids this, but typically at a cost in accuracy. We study how to achieve accurate and memory efficient local learning in transformers. For each transformer block, we train a local proxy network to predict the global network out","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T08:41:25Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23145.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很基础的问题：能不能在 Transformer 上用“局部学习”替代标准端到端反向传播，同时把显存占用显著降下来，而精度不要掉得太多。标准 backprop 的瓶颈不只是算力，更是 activation 必须跨整网保存到反传结束，这让深层模型、长序列和大 batch 都直接受限。已有 local learning 方法往往用局部辅助损失训练每一层或每一块，但这些局部目标和全局任务梯度之间缺少严格对应，所以一旦网络变深，误差会快速累积。作者要解决的核心不是再设计一个经验上可用的 local loss，而是构造一种有明确梯度近似含义的局部替代：每个 block 只看本地激活，通过一个 proxy 去预测全局输出，再用 proxy 内部反传来近似真实的全局梯度。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在训练系统和优化接口，而不在直接改进语言建模目标。它给出了一条不同于 activation checkpointing、recompute、ZeRO、低精度压缩的路线：不是继续为 backprop 节省内存，而是从训练信号本身改写反传依赖。这个方向如果能继续成立，价值在三点。第一，它把“局部更新”从经验技巧推进到可分析的梯度估计框架，至少在深线性网络和残差占优的非线性网络里有理论支撑。第二，它对长上下文和深层 Transformer 的 memory wall 有直接相关性，因为 locality 的定义就是一次只保留一个 block 的激活。第三，它在 LLM 微调场景里已经显示出比零阶方法更好的精度/内存/时间折中，这说明它不是只在小模型玩具实验里成立。不过要注意，当前证据还停留在 memory-efficient finetuning，不是大规模从头预训练；因此它更像一个值得跟踪的训练范式候选，而不是已经可替代标准预训练 recipe 的方案。","technical_approach":"方法叫 ProxyProp。做法是把深层 Transformer 划分成多个 gradient-isolated blocks。第 l 个 block 的输入是 h_{l-1}，输出是 h_l。标准 backprop 需要通过后续所有 block 把最终损失对 h_l 的梯度传回来；ProxyProp 则给每个 block 额外挂一个本地 proxy network，输入 h_l，目标是预测全模型最终输出，训练信号来自对全局输出的蒸馏。然后该 block 的参数更新不再依赖真实下游网络的反传，而是把损失先对 proxy 输出求导，再经由 proxy 对 h_l 反传，得到对 block 参数的局部梯度估计。\n\n关键点不只是“加一个辅助头”，而是作者把 proxy 设计成对全局 Jacobian 的局部估计器。在线性网络里，若 proxy 是线性的，最优 proxy 会隐式学到从当前 block 表示到最终输出的期望线性映射，因此局部梯度与真实全局梯度一致。对非线性残差网络，作者强调 residual parameterization 很重要：当 skip connection 主导时，block 到输出的映射更接近恒等加小扰动，proxy 的梯度误差可被界定；在初始化附近，局部梯度甚至可以与真实梯度精确对齐。这个分析解释了为什么方法更适合现代残差 Transformer，而不是任意深层非残差网络。\n\n训练流程上，block 在前向后即可立刻更新，不需要等待整网 backward，因此满足 decoupled / unlocked learning 的定义。内存节省来自两部分：一是不再保存整网 activation；二是每次只需保留单个 block 及其 proxy 的局部计算图。与传统 local loss 方法相比，区别在于 ProxyProp 的目标不是让每层直接完成分类或重建，而是逼近“如果继续经过后续网络，最终会对输出产生什么影响”。与 MeZO 这类零阶优化相比，ProxyProp 仍然使用梯度信息，只是梯度来自局部代理，因此在相近内存预算下通常更高效。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，实验分成两类。第一类是视觉 Transformer 上的监督学习，用来系统比较不同 block 划分下的 memory-accuracy trade-off。作者的结论是，ProxyProp 在多种 partition 方式下都优于已有局部学习算法：同等内存下精度更高，或同等精度下内存更低。这里真正值得看的不是单一最好点，而是随着 block 数增加、locality 变强时，性能退化是否平滑；作者声称 ProxyProp 的退化更慢，说明梯度近似比传统 local objectives 更稳。\n\n第二类是 LLM 的 memory-efficient finetuning。作者把 ProxyProp 和 MeZO 做对比，结论是：在可比显存成本下，ProxyProp 的下游性能更高；即便 MeZO 使用约 8 倍计算时间，结果仍不如 ProxyProp。这个对比很关键，因为零阶方法常被当作极低显存微调的备选，但代价是方差大、收敛慢。ProxyProp 说明“局部一阶近似”可能是更合理的低显存路径。\n\n不过，当前公开内容里没有足够完整的数值表来复述所有具体数字，例如 ViT 的具体模型规模、数据集、不同 partition 的精度差值、LLM 微调所用参数量和任务集合。阅读时应重点核对实验章节中的三类细节：1）显存统计是否包含 optimizer state、activation、proxy 额外开销；2）时间开销是 wall-clock 还是 FLOPs；3）LLM 微调是否只覆盖 PEFT/adapter 场景，还是更新全参数。若是后者，意义会更大。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓理论，再看实验边界。优先看 Introduction 里对 locality、decoupling、unlocked training 的定义，因为这决定了作者到底在和谁比较。然后直接看理论部分关于两件事的证明：一是深线性网络中 proxy 为什么能精确恢复全局梯度；二是非线性残差网络里误差上界为什么依赖 residual parameterization 和 skip dominance。这两点决定方法不是拍脑袋的辅助蒸馏。\n\n实验部分建议重点看：1）ViT 上随 block 划分变化的精度-内存曲线；2）proxy 结构的消融，尤其是线性 proxy 与更复杂 proxy 的差别；3）LLM finetuning 与 MeZO 的对比设置是否公平。预计 30-45 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。如果只想判断是否值得继续跟踪，抓住一个问题即可：ProxyProp 的收益主要来自“更好的局部目标”，还是来自“允许更激进的内存-并行策略”。如果是前者，它更像优化算法；如果是后者，它可能会和现有训练系统技术形成互补。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question: can transformers be trained with a local learning rule that materially reduces activation memory, without paying the usual large accuracy penalty of layer-wise or block-wise local objectives? Standard backpropagation requires storing activations across the whole network until gradients are propagated from the final loss. That creates a hard memory bottleneck for deep models and long sequences. Prior local learning methods usually optimize each layer or block with an auxiliary local loss, but those losses are only loosely related to the true end-to-end gradient, so performance degrades quickly with depth. The paper targets this gap directly. Instead of inventing another heuristic local objective, it tries to construct a local surrogate whose gradient is an explicit approximation to the global backpropagated gradient.","importance":"$12","technical_approach":"$13","experiments":"$14","reading_guide":"$15","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"探讨用局部代理网络替代全局 BP 以节省显存，理论分析有意思（残差连接下的误差界），但目前只在 CIFAR-100 的 ViT 上验证，离 LLM 预训练的 scale 还有距离，可关注其理论推导。","en":"Explores using local proxy networks to replace global BP for memory savings. Success then requires that some candidate is correct and that the verifier picks it. The first grows predictably with the number of samples; the second does not, because real verifiers (unit tests, majority votes, LLM judges, reward models) accept wrong answers, reject right ones, and make correlated mistakes. With a fi","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T10:28:07Z","category_id":"scaling","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23255.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在“多采样 + 验证器选优”的 test-time compute 框架下，给定固定推理预算，应该把算力分配给：采多少个候选（sample）、是否混合多个生成模型（mix）、以及花多少调用在验证/复核/阈值选择上（verify）？难点在于真实验证器并不完美：会误接收错误答案、误拒绝正确答案，而且错误高度相关（同一个看似合理的错解会骗过很多 judge；不同生成器也会卡在同一批难题）。论文要解决的是：在这种“生成 + 选择”系统里，如何用一个小规模标注 pilot 来校准一个可解释的成功概率模型，并据此做预算最优的配置选择。","importance":"对基座研究的影响不在“改预训练目标”，而在 pretrain↔posttrain 闭环与推理侧 scaling 的可量化决策：\n- 把 test-time scaling 从经验法则（多采样、强模型、更多 judge）推进到“可校准的资源分配问题”。这对 RL/PRM/RM、LLM-as-judge、unit test 等验证信号的使用方式有直接指导意义。\n- 明确指出 verified success 会被 verifier precision 上限卡死，且相关性会让 coverage→success 的转化严重失真；这会反过来影响训练信号设计（例如训练 verifier/PRM 时要关注相关错误与阈值校准，而不只是平均准确率）。\n- 方法层面给出一个可落地的 planner（SPLIT），用小 pilot 做经验贝叶斯估计并输出预算分配；对做 agent/coding 系统的人是“能直接用”的组件，对做预训练/对齐的人是“如何度量与建模 verifier 缺陷”的参考框架。\n整体贡献量级：不是提出新 benchmark，而是给出一个带证明的成功概率公式 + 一套可复现的校准与规划流程，并在多任务上展示接近 hindsight-optimal 的分配效果。","technical_approach":"SPLIT 的核心是把最终成功率写成两项乘积并显式建模“选择”而非只建模“生成覆盖率”：\n- success = P(存在正确候选) × P(在存在时被选中 | 存在)。\n第一项随采样数 N 增长通常平滑（常见幂律），但第二项取决于 verifier 的真/假接收率与相关性结构。\n\n论文在 Section 3 推导了两类常见 selector 的“精确成功率公式”：\n1) 在所有被 verifier 接收的候选中均匀随机选一个（uniform among accepted）。\n2) 选择 verifier 打分最高的候选（argmax / top-scoring）。\n\n公式中显式纳入三类相关性来源（对应现实系统的三种“重复犯错”）：\n- 任务难度异质性：不同题目的可解概率差异很大，导致 coverage 不能用 i.i.d. 假设外推。\n- 跨生成器共享盲点：不同模型在同一批题上同时失败，用 Gaussian copula 建模跨生成器相关。\n- 重复 verifier 调用相关：同一候选被多次 judge 时，误判并非独立，用“候选级 acceptance propensity”建模。\n\nSection 4 用经验贝叶斯从小规模标注 pilot 估计上述成分，并给出“verifier 准确率随任务难度变化”的变体（避免把 judge 的准确率在任务上简单 pooling 造成偏差）。\n\n规划器（planner）在给定成本预算下搜索配置：选哪些生成器、各采样数、验证调用数/阈值等，使预测成功率最大。论文还给出 regret 与“微分误差（differential forecast error）”相关的界（Proposition 4），强调不需要绝对精确的概率预测也能做出接近最优的分配。\n\n额外机制分析（Section 6.3）把一些经验现象变成可判别条件：\n- 何时 majority-of-k 复核会降低可达 precision（给出精确条件），并实证 LLM judges 在 MATH-500 满足该条件而 GPQA 不满足。\n- 生成器越强 verifier false-positive 越高的现象，被解释为“打分型 verifier 的阈值校准效应”，并在 HumanEval+/MBPP+/CodeContests 上复现。","experiments":"实验覆盖五类 allocation 问题（真实或模拟 verifier）以及一个 agent 阈值选择问题；核心结论与数字（来自全文引言给出的结果概述）：\n- 在未参与校准的任务上，SPLIT 选出的配置与 hindsight-optimal 的差距在 0.02–0.8 个 agent 系统池）。","reading_guide":"精读建议（2–3 小时，值得逐段推）：\n1) Section 3：两类 selector 的精确成功率公式与证明思路。这里决定了 SPLIT 不是“拟合曲线”，而是把 verifier 错误与相关性写进可计算的目标。\n2) Section 4：经验贝叶斯估计细节，尤其是“judge 准确率与任务难度耦合”的建模方式；这部分直接决定 的估计方法。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Under a fixed inference-time budget, how should one allocate compute between (i) sampling more candidate solutions, (ii) mixing candidates from multiple generators, and (iii) spending compute on verification (more judges, re-checks, thresholds) when the verifier is imperfect and errors are correlated? The paper targets the practical gap that coverage scales smoothly with samples, but verified success often saturates far below coverage due to verifier false accepts/rejects and shared failure modes.","importance":"The contribution is not a new pretraining objective; it is a rigorous decision framework for test-time scaling that feeds back into how we should think about verifiers (unit tests, LLM judges, reward models) used in post-training and agentic systems.\n- It reframes “sample more” into an allocation problem where the bottleneck is frequently selection under imperfect verifiers, not generation coverage.\n- It makes verifier limitations explicit: verified success can be capped by verifier precision, and correlation can destroy the naive i.i.d. gains practitioners assume.\n- SPLIT is operational: with a small labeled pilot, it outputs near-optimal budget splits across generators/samples/verification, and its modeling choices (difficulty coupling, cross-generator correlation, correlated judge calls) are directly relevant to RM/PRM and LLM-as-judge pipelines.\nNet impact: a provable success formula + a calibrated planner that consistently beats fixed heuristics across settings.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"Deep-read path (2–3 hours):\n1) Section 3: the exact success formulas for the two selection rules; verifier。如果同一模型的错误在采样、推理深度和自评中高度相关，那么这些信号不能按独立证据处理。更可靠的训练与评测闭环需要显式引入异质性：不同模型、不同训练谱系、不同解题路径，或者至少避免让同一模型同时当生成器、裁判和证据来源。贡献量级属于概念清晰、实验支撑较完整的方法论文，对 test-time scaling、reasoning data selection、RL verifier MMLU-Pro、GPQA-Diamond 等数据。第二是 initial-answer persistence bias：模型先直接作答，再进行推理后作答，后者仍强烈受初始答案影响；初始错答经常在后续推理中被保留。第三是 longer-reasoning preference bias：当模型评价自己早期答案和后期答案时，会系统性偏向后期、更长、更复杂的答案。文中报告在 disagreement case 中，模型超过 95% 选择 later-stage answer，而且错误时仍然高置信。Dia 的设计对应这三个观察：先比较中间阶段预测和最终预测。如果二者一致，直接返回模型答案；如果不一致，把该题交给一个小型异质模型 panel。Panel 中的模型独立解题，不看 base model 的两个候选答案，最后只在答案层面聚合。这个协议与 multi-agent judge 的差别很重要：它不让裁判接触候选推理文本，减少被长度、表述和 base model 锚点带偏的机会。","experiments":"实验覆盖 7 个 benchmark 和两个 base model：Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B。Dia 在 14 个设置中的 12 个超过 full chain-of-thought。一个具体结果是 MuSiQue 上从 59.0% 提升到 66.0%。Dia 平均只在约 17% 样本上触发外部 panel，因此不是把所有题都交给 ensemble。与每题调用所有 agent 的 fully-connected multi-agent baseline 相比，Dia 最多减少 47.2% token。聚合方式的消融也有信息量：在 Qwen3-4B 的 MuSiQue 上，中心化 judge 路由 independent agent outputs 得到 60.67%，而直接答案聚合达到 66.00%。这说明问题不只是是否使用多个模型，还包括是否让一个 judge 看到候选并做带偏的比较。诊断实验还显示，同模型 SC 的错误会集中到主导错答，推理后的答案会继承初始答案，自评会偏向更长的后期推理。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Section 2 的三组 bias 诊断，重点关注 C_q 的定义、SC@20 数据构造时，能否降低同源自举带来的偏置。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether additional test-time computation from the same reasoning model should be treated as additional evidence. Its answer is largely no. Repeated samples, longer reasoning, and self-evaluation can preserve the same model-specific error bias, so more same-model compute may reinforce a wrong belief instead of correcting it.","importance":"The main value is the clean separation between compute and evidence. This matters beyond inference-time methods. Many reasoning-data and RL pipelines rely on same-model sampling, self-consistency, self-critique, or model-written rationales as if they were independent signals. The paper shows why that assumption is fragile. For pretraining and post-training interfaces, it suggests a practical rule: when using model-generated reasoning as supervision or verification, diversity in model provenance and aggregation protocol matters. A model should not simultaneously be the generator, the critic, and the source of supposedly independent evidence.","technical_approach":"$18","experiments":"The evaluation uses seven benchmarks and two base models, Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B. The arbitration panel is activated on only about 17% of examples, so the method is selective rather than a full ensemble. Compared with a fully connected multi-agent baseline that invokes all agents on all questions, Dia uses up to 47.2% fewer tokens. The aggregation ablation is important: on MuSiQue with Qwen3-4B, routing independent agent outputs through a centralized judge gives 60.67%, while direct answer aggregation gives 66.00%. This supports the claim that the protocol matters, not just the presence of multiple models. The diagnostic experiments further show concentrated wrong answers under same-model sampling, persistence from initial answers to reasoned answers, and a strong self-evaluation bias toward later reasoning.","reading_guide":"Read closely. While Maximal Update Parametrization ($μ$P) allows for zero-shot hyperparameter transfer in standard training, we show that it fails under DP constraints due to the distinct spectral properties of high-dimensional noise versus low-rank gradients. In this work, w","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T05:51:00Z","category_id":"scaling","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22976.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CR","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作处理的是差分隐私优化中的超参数迁移问题。标准 μP 可以把小模型上调好的学习率迁移到大宽度模型，但在 DP-SGD、DP-Adam、DP-Muon 这类带 per-sample clipping 和高维高斯噪声的训练中会失效。原因不是简单的噪声变大，而是 DP 噪声与梯度在谱结构上不同：梯度通常呈低秩或有效低秩结构，而注入噪声是高维满秩矩阵，宽度变大后其谱范数按随机矩阵规律增长，破坏了 μP 原来依赖的层间更新尺度稳定性。","importance":"对常规 LLM 预训练的直接影响有限，因为大规模通用预训练很少全程使用严格 DP 优化。但它对两类问题有启发：一是隐私约束下的持续预训练、私有数据微调、企业内部数据训练；二是所有“噪声主导”的大模型优化，包括零阶优化、强噪声梯度估计、带裁剪的训练稳定性分析。贡献主要是把 μP 的宽度缩放逻辑扩展到 DP 场景，并指出不能只看 Frobenius 范数或更新均方尺度，还要看噪声的谱范数和信噪比条件。这个视角对分析大宽度模型中的优化噪声很有用。","technical_approach":"方法称为 DP-μP。核心做法是把 layer-wise clipping、DP noise injection 和 μP 的 feature learning 条件放在同一个尺度分析框架里。作者先用随机矩阵理论刻画噪声主导区间：高维 Gaussian 噪声矩阵是满秩的，谱范数随维度增长；而训练梯度信号通常集中在较少方向上，谱结构更稳定。这个差异导致标准 μP 的学习率迁移规则在 DP 下不再保持层级更新平衡。随后作者推导出一个 layer-wise SNR 条件，用来约束每层学习率、裁剪阈值、噪声规模、宽度之间的关系。该条件分别用于 DP-SGD、DP-Adam 和 DP-Muon，并区分 clipping-only 与完整 DP 设置。与原始 μP 相比，DP-μP 不是只要求参数更新在宽度上保持同阶，而是额外要求隐私噪声经过优化器预条件后不会在谱范数意义上压过可学习信号到无法迁移的程度。","experiments":"实验重点是验证零样本学习率迁移和 coordinate check。作者在小模型上寻找最优学习率，然后按 DP-μP 的缩放规则迁移到更大模型，并与逐模型单独调参的基线比较。结果显示，DP-μP 可以在 DP-SGD、DP-Adam、DP-Muon 等优化器下保持较稳定的层级坐标尺度，并让从小到大的学习率迁移达到或接近逐规模调参的性能，有些设置中超过单独调参基线。给出的全文片段没有暴露完整表格数值、具体模型规模和数据集细节，因此不应把实验量级解读为已经覆盖 LLM 级别训练。更准确的定位是：它在受控深度学习设置里验证了 DP 噪声下 μP 缩放规则需要重写。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 到 35 分钟。重点看三处：第一，Introduction 中关于标准 μP 在 DP 下失效的解释，尤其是低秩梯度与满秩噪声的谱差异；第二，推导 SNR condition 和 spectral condition 的小节，理解学习率、宽度、裁剪阈值、噪声规模如何耦合；第三，DP-SGD、DP-Adam、DP-Muon 的缩放规则表和 coordinate check 图。若关注 LLM 预训练，可以跳过部分 DP 会计细节，把重点放在噪声主导优化和超参数迁移的尺度条件上。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies hyperparameter transfer for differentially private optimization. Standard μP supports learning-rate transfer across width in non-private training, but it breaks once per-sample clipping and high-dimensional DP noise are introduced. The failure is not just caused by a larger noise magnitude. The key issue is spectral: gradients tend to have low-rank or effectively low-rank structure, while injected Gaussian noise is full-rank, and its spectral norm grows with model width.","importance":"The direct relevance to full-scale public LLM pretraining is moderate, since strict DP optimization is rarely used for the entire pretraining run. The useful part is the analysis of noisy large-width optimization. It applies naturally to private continual pretraining, private fine-tuning, and potentially to other noise-dominated regimes such as zeroth-order optimization. The main contribution is to show that μP-style transfer cannot be reasoned about only through update magnitude; layer-wise signal-to-noise and spectral scaling also matter.","technical_approach":"The proposed framework, DP-μP, extends μP by incorporating layer-wise clipping and DP noise. The analysis first characterizes the noise-dominated regime using random matrix theory. Gaussian privacy noise behaves as a high-dimensional full-rank matrix, while the useful gradient signal has a more stable and lower-rank spectrum. This mismatch changes the correct width scaling. The authors then derive a layer-wise SNR condition that determines when learning-rate transfer remains valid. The condition is instantiated for DP-SGD, DP-Adam, and DP-Muon, covering both clipping-only and full DP settings. Compared with vanilla μP, the framework asks not only whether update coordinates remain stable, but also whether the useful signal remains learnable after optimizer preconditioning and privacy noise injection.","experiments":"The empirical section focuses on coordinate checks and zero-shot learning-rate transfer. Learning rates tuned on smaller models are transferred to larger models using the DP-μP scaling rules and compared with individually tuned baselines. The reported results show stable coordinate behavior and transferred learning rates that match or sometimes exceed separately tuned runs across DP-SGD, DP-Adam, and DP-Muon settings. The provided text does not expose the full numerical tables, model sizes, or datasets, so the safe interpretation is that the evidence supports the scaling analysis in controlled settings rather than demonstrating LLM-scale private pretraining.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 35 minutes. DP accounting details can be read lightly unless the goal is private training itself.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看它如何把 μP 失效归因到噪声谱而不是口头上的“DP 更难训”；先看理论推导和 transfer 曲线，是否能外推到 noisy pretrain 是关键。","en":"Worth reading for its spectral explanation of why μP breaks under DP rather than the vague 'DP is harder'; focus on the theory and transfer curves."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Tianze Wang","Zhiqi Bu","Linjun Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["muP","differential-privacy","scaling-laws","hyperparameter-transfer","optimization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22976","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.22976","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.23337","title":"Minimax-optimal Scaling Laws in Ridge Regression via Transfer Learning","abstract":"Pretrained models are often reused across downstream tasks, motivating the question of whether this transfer learning procedure can provide a substantial performance improvement, namely, in terms of the scaling law. H - c。单个 agent 的能力不是常数，而是 reasoning budget g 的函数：g 太小时不能完成推理，g 增加后准确率上升，超过某个范围后收益变小。这个能力曲线用可拟合的 thinking-gain function φ 从 held-out calibration data 中估计。然后论文用允许相关错误的 Condorcet jury model 聚合投票，定义 per-answer margin δ、group accuracy A(n)、effective sample size n_eff，以及 synergy index S(n)=δ√n_eff。这样可以在不完整 sweep debate 配置的情况下，根据单 agent cap sweep 和初始轮的错误相关性估计，闭式预测最优 group size n*。框架还扩展到 heterogeneous model set，不只决定用多少 agent，也决定纳入哪些模型。","experiments":"关键结果集中在预算受限的 debate 选择上。论文称，预测的 group size 与 per-cell oracle 的准确率差距为 1.4%，每个 cell 使用 26 到 79 个 calibration questions；对比之下，debate-calibrated rules 在相同开销下只用一两个问题，但稳定性较弱。与 self-consistency 的对比很直接：在 980 个 matched operating points 中，self-consistency 持平或优于 deployed debate 的比例为 93%；paired test 中 debate 显著领先的比例只有 0.5%。差距在 tight budget 下最大，在 loose budget 下缩小，因为通信 token 占总预算比例下降。论文还指出 self-refinement 每轮都要重读自己的上一版输出，却没有 voting amplification，因此随着轮次增加，准确率下降更快。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 分钟。先看问题设定中 W、H、c、g 的预算定义，确认它和常见“固定每个 agent 生成长度”的分析有什么差别。然后看 ability curve φ 和相关错误 Condorcet 模型，这两部分决定 n* 的闭式预测是否可信。实验部分重点看 self-consistency 对 debate 的 matched-budget 比较，以及 calibration cost 与 oracle gap。若研究方向是预训练，可把它作为 test-time compute 分配的参考，而不是语言建模目标论文来读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical trade-off in reasoning systems with shared context: reading peer responses or previous drafts consumes the same token window that could otherwise be used for additional reasoning. Under a fixed token budget, adding agents or refinement rounds can reduce, rather than increase, the amount of useful thinking.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is useful for reasoning-time scaling, post-training evaluation, and the design of agentic pipelines. Many debate and self-refinement analyses treat communication and reasoning as separate resources. In deployed LLM calls they share the same context budget. The paper gives a calibrated model for when communication is worth its token cost, and why self-consistency often competes well with multi-agent debate under matched budgets. It is relevant for researchers studying inference-time compute allocation, long-context usage, RL data generation, and agent evaluation.","technical_approach":"$19","experiments":"The reported results emphasize tight-budget regimes. Debate-calibrated rules use only one or two questions at matched spend, but provide weaker operating-point selection. The comparison with self-consistency is especially informative: across 980 matched operating points, self-consistency matches or beats deployed debate in 93% of cases, while a paired test finds debate significantly ahead in only 0.5%. c","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T07:12:41Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23036.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 looped language model 应该如何设计。Looped model 通过重复使用同一组 Transformer blocks，把“存储参数量”和“逻辑计算深度”分开：显存和参数存储接近小模型，前向计算路径接近深模型。已有 Ouro 和 Huginn 两条设计路线效果不同，但它们同时改变了循环位置、输入注入方式、循环状态初始化和状态组织，难以判断收益来自哪里。作者把这些因素拆成三个轴：iteration envelope、input injection、latent-state organization，并在相同参数量、相同逻辑深度、相同 token budget 下逐项消融。","importance":"这是一篇对架构预训练有直接参考价值的工作。它不是提出一个单点技巧，而是把 looped Transformer 的设计空间拆开，并用从头预训练的受控实验回答哪些设计真的起作用。结论很清楚：把循环层放在 prelude–recurrent block–coda 的 sandwich 结构中，是最稳定的收益来源；反复注入 prelude 表示有助于知识检索和上下文利用，但对定量推理可能有副作用；随机初始化循环状态和分离高/低状态没有稳定收益。对未来“用更多计算换更少 resident parameters”的模型很有参考意义，尤其是在显存容量和带宽比算力更稀缺的硬件趋势下。","technical_approach":"作者先建立一个统一记号，把 looped model 写成 Pθ、Rθ、Cθ 三部分。Pθ 是只执行一次的 prelude，Rθ 是多次复用的 recurrent core，Cθ 是只执行一次的 coda。输入 token 先映射为 embedding，再经过可选的 prelude 得到固定表示 e。循环状态 z_t 在每一步通过共享的 Rθ 更新，可选择是否通过 Wθ 把固定输入表示重新写入循环流。Ouro 风格可以表示为 P、W、C 都是 identity，z_0 直接等于 token embedding，完整 28 层 stack 重复 4 次，即 R28^4。Huginn 风格则采用非平凡的 prelude 和 coda，在中间 recurrent core 上循环，并在每次迭代注入 prelude 表示，例如 P8 R12^8 C8。这样做的好处是把输入处理、循环计算、输出整合分开：prelude 负责形成稳定的上下文表征，recurrent core 负责反复计算，coda 负责把循环后的状态转成最终预测。与直接重复整栈相比，sandwich 结构减少了循环对原始 embedding 和最终输出层的干扰。","experiments":"核心受控实验把 looped models 固定在 730M stored parameters，并让 unrolled computation 对应 2.9B parameter applications。所有 looped 变体存储 28 个 Transformer blocks，但通过循环形成 112 层逻辑深度。Ouro 基线是 R28^4，即 28 层完整栈重复 4 次。Huginn 变体是 P8 R12^8 C8，即 8 层 prelude、12 层 recurrent core 循环 8 次、8 层 coda，总逻辑深度同样为 112。两个 feedforward 锚点分别是 D28，匹配 resident 参数量；D112，匹配逻辑深度但拥有独立参数。消融结果显示，iteration envelope 的改变最关键：prelude–loop–coda sandwich 在数学推理、上下文抽取、推理密集任务上稳定优于直接重复整栈。input injection 主要提升知识检索、上下文恢复和遵循 prompt 中给定规格的能力，但在一些定量推理任务上会带来干扰。随机状态初始化没有一致收益。分离 high/low recurrent states 增加复杂度，但收益不可靠。组合所有设计后，完整 Huginn 在十个 dense benchmarks 上都优于 Ouro。作者还把结论迁移到 MoE：在 resident 参数和 active 参数匹配的 MoE 设置下，Huginn 仍优于 Ouro，expert load 更均衡，不同循环步会使用不同专家，并且接近更大 feedforward MoE 的效果，同时存储参数少得多。","reading_guide":"建议精读。优先看三个部分。第一，Preliminary 的统一形式，它把 Ouro 和 Huginn 放在同一个 P/R/C 框架里，是理解后续消融的关键。第二，Controlled Ablations of Looped-Model Design，重点看 Q1、Q2、Q3：Q1 判断 sandwich topology 是否有效，Q2 判断输入注入带来的收益和副作用，Q3 判断随机初始化与状态拆分是否值得保留。第三，MoE 迁移实验，重点看 expert load 和跨 iteration 的专家使用差异，因为这关系到 looped MoE 是否只是省参数，还是确实学会在不同循环步执行不同计算。若时间有限，先读架构定义、730M/2.9B 对齐设置、dense benchmark 总表和 MoE expert 分析图。值得深挖的问题包括：循环次数增加时是否存在训练不稳定或 residual drift；input injection 为什么会改善上下文利用但干扰部分数学任务；looped MoE 中专家是否承担了类似“迭代阶段”的分工。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks how looped language models should be built. These models reuse the same Transformer blocks across logical depth, separating stored parameters from the amount of computation applied at inference or training time. Prior designs such as Ouro and Huginn differ along several axes at once: where recurrence is placed, whether the input is repeatedly injected, how the recurrent state is initialized, and whether multiple recurrent states are used. The paper disentangles these choices under matched parameter count, logical depth, and training budget.","importance":"This is directly relevant to pretraining architecture research. The value is not a single new module, but a controlled decomposition of the design space for recurrent-depth Transformers. The main result is that topology matters most: a prelude–recurrent-core–coda sandwich is consistently better than looping over the entire stack. Repeated input injection helps retrieval and context use, but can interfere with some quantitative reasoning tasks. Random recurrent-state initialization and separate high/low states do not provide reliable gains. The study is timely because looped models trade compute for memory, which matches the hardware trend where arithmetic throughput grows faster than memory capacity and bandwidth.","technical_approach":"The authors introduce a common formulation with three components: a prelude Pθ, a tied recurrent body Rθ, and a coda Cθ. The prelude and coda are executed once, while the recurrent body is reused for multiple iterations. A fixed representation e from the prelude can be written back into the recurrent stream at each step through an input-write map Wθ. In this notation, an Ouro-style model has identity prelude, input write, and coda, initializes the recurrent stream from token embeddings, and repeats the full stack. A Huginn-style model places recurrence inside a feedforward envelope, injects the prelude representation during the loop, and may vary the recurrent-state design. This formulation isolates three choices: iteration envelope, input injection, and latent-state organization. The strongest design is the sandwich topology, because it lets the model separate input encoding, iterative computation, and final readout rather than repeatedly applying the entire stack to all stages.","experiments":"$1a","reading_guide":"Read closely. Start with the common P/R/C formulation, since it makes the Ouro-to-Huginn ablations interpretable. Then focus on the controlled ablation section: Q1 for the iteration envelope, Q2 for input injection, and Q3 for recurrent-state initialization and organization. The 730M stored-parameter versus 2.9B unrolled-compute setup is important because it defines the compute-memory tradeoff. The MoE section is also worth careful reading, especially the expert-load and per-iteration expert-usage analyses. Good questions to track while reading are whether deeper recurrence causes residual drift, why input injection helps context use but may disrupt quantitative reasoning, and whether MoE experts begin to specialize by loop iteration.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇该认真看：它把 looped LM 里最脏的混杂变量拆开了，若 setup 真匹配，Huginn 的 sandwich 设计应立刻进内部 ablation。","en":"Read this carefully: it isolates the dirtiest confounds in looped LMs, and if the setup is truly matched, the Huginn sandwich should go straight into internal ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":["CMU","MBZUAI","xAI"],"authors":["Benhao Huang","Chufan Shi","Junlin Chen","Shicheng Wen","Zhengzhong Liu","Eric Xing","Xuezhe Ma"],"notable_authors":["Eric Xing","Xuezhe Ma","Zhengzhong Liu"],"tags":["looped-language-models","weight-sharing","recurrent-transformer","moe","architecture"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23036","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.23036","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.23122","title":"LED: Improving Masked Diffusion Language Models by Lookahead Editing","abstract":"Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, enabling non-autoregressive text generation through parallel decoding. Existing dLLMs can be broadly categorized into two families: masked diffusion models (MDMs) and uniform diffusion models (UDMs) In this work, we theoretically show that MDMs are easier to optimize due ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T08:29:07Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23122.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是 diffusion LM 里一个很核心的结构性矛盾：masked diffusion model（MDM）训练简单、目标干净、优化稳定，但一旦 token 被 commit 就不能再改，计算表达能力受限；uniform diffusion model（UDM）允许 token 在去噪过程中反复改写，表达力更强，但训练目标里会引入依赖模型本身的 schedule matching UDM 的差异讲成了两个层面：一是优化层面的 ELBO 结构差异，二是计算表达能力层面的 circuit complexity 差异。前者解释为什么很多 dLLM 训练起来不顺；后者解释为什么“能不能改写已生成 token”不是实现细节，而是模型能力边界。LED 给出的答案也很有代表性：不直接把 rewritability 塞进 forward process，而是在 MDM 外围加一个可反复编辑的 proposal branch。对后续做 masked/any-order LM、diffusion LM、甚至 AR+iterative refinement 混合范式的人，这个设计空间很有启发。","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"实验基于 Qwen2.5-7B 和 Qwen3-8B 两个底座，使用相同训练集分别做标准 MDM SFT 和 LED SFT，对比覆盖数学、代码、科学共八个 benchmark。主结果很直接：LED SFT 相比标准 MDM SFT，Qwen2.5-7B 平均提升 +11.4 分，Qwen3-8B 平均提升 +7.7 分。论文还强调，仅靠 SFT 的 LED-8B 就超过了 LLaDA2.1-16B Q-Mode，而后者使用了 continued pretraining、SFT 和 RL 的完整流程。这一点说明 LED 带来的收益不是小幅调参，而是能明显改变 dLLM 的有效能力边界。\n\n更值得看的是实验背后的机制验证。作者比较了不同 lookahead 编辑步数、proposal 质量、以及 proposal/commit 交互方式，结论大致是：适度的 lookahead revision 能持续提升结果，但收益并非无限增长，说明 proposal branch 的作用是提供可修正的中间状态，而不是无上限地堆迭代。论文还展示了模型会学到 selective editing：对高置信 proposal token 倾向保留，对错误 token 倾向改写，这支持了训练目标确实在学“编辑”，而不是简单忽略 proposal。\n\n理论部分的复杂度分析也有配套任务或构造实验，用来说明 rewritable state 对层次化计算更有利。即便这些实验不一定直接转化成通用 benchmark 分数，它们仍然帮助解释为什么 LED 在数学和代码任务上收益更明显：这些任务更依赖中间结构的反复修正，而不是一次性提交。\n\n如果从预训练研究角度看，最重要的数字不是单个榜单成绩，而是：在同样底座、同样数据、同样 SFT 预算下，仅改变建模和解码结构就能拿到 7–11 分量级的平均提升，这说明 MDM 的表达瓶颈确实是实在存在的。","reading_guide":"这篇建议精读，顺序可以按“理论动机 → 方法结构 → 实验验证”来走。\n\n先看引言和理论部分关于 ELBO 分解的章节。重点理解为什么 MDM 的 schedule matching term 为零，而 UDM 会多出模型相关项。这部分决定了作者为什么不直接往 UDM 方向走。\n\n然后看 circuit complexity 那一节。即使不逐条跟证明，也要抓住结论：MDM 的 commit-only 机制限制了计算深度，rewritable state 才能支持更层次化的并行计算。这里是全文最不常见、但也最有解释力的部分。\n\n接着看 LED 方法图和训练/推理流程。要把 proposal stage、commit stage、lookahead steps、跨 stage token 保留与修正的关系彻底看明白。很多读者会把它误解成“先草稿再修订”，但论文真正做的是在每个 commit 阶段内部维护一个可反复编辑的分支。\n\n实验部分优先看主表、与 LLaDA2.1-16B Q-Mode 的对比、lookahead 步数消融、proposal 质量相关分析，以及展示 selective editing 行为的案例或统计图。这些内容最能判断 LED 的收益来自哪里。\n\n如果时间有限，至少读引言、理论结论图、方法图、主结果表和关键消融，约 40–60 分钟能抓住主线。若准备把这个方向用于新型 LM 研究，建议连附录一起看，尤其是复杂度证明、训练细节、以及 proposal 生成和条件注入的实现方式。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a structural trade-off in diffusion language models. Masked diffusion models (MDMs) are easy to optimize because their mask-based transition schedule is simple and model-independent, but once a token is committed it cannot be revised, which limits computational expressivity. Uniform diffusion models (UDMs) allow iterative rewriting of tokens and are therefore more expressive, but they are substantially harder to train because the objective includes an additional model-dependent schedule-matching term. The paper asks whether one can recover the benefits of iterative revision without giving up the optimization simplicity of MDMs.","importance":"This is a must-read for anyone working on alternative language modeling paradigms. Its contribution is not just a new decoding heuristic. It gives a clean two-level explanation of the MDM-versus-UDM trade-off: an optimization difference through path-space ELBO decomposition, and an expressivity difference through circuit complexity. The first explains why some diffusion LMs are much harder to train; the second explains why token rewritability is not an implementation detail but a capability boundary. LED is interesting because it does not force rewritability into the diffusion process itself. Instead, it wraps an editable proposal branch around an MDM-style commit process. That design space is relevant not only for diffusion LMs, but also for masked, any-order, and hybrid iterative-refinement language models.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"$1e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"diffusion 文本里少见的“解码机制”实质改动：把迭代修订塞回 MDM 日程；先看理论对 MDM/UDM 取舍的刻画，再核对质量-算力曲线是否真划算。","en":"A rare decoding-mechanism change that tries to graft iterative revision onto an MDM schedule; read the MDM-vs-UDM analysis first, then sanity-check the quality–compute curve to see if the trade-off is real."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yuyan Zhou","Zeyuan Yang","Yuxuan Song","Zhicheng Cai","Hongli Yu","Weiying Ma","Hao Zhou","James Kwok"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-LM","masked-diffusion","non-autoregressive-decoding","iterative-editing","text-generation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23122","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.23122","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.23531","title":"PONDER: Learning to Generate in the Right Order","abstract":"We introduce PONDER, a method that learns in which order an image generator should fill in content and which of its earlier predictions to revise. Masked generative models construct a sample by repeatedly filling in subsets of missing variables. The order of these partial predictions determines the context available in subsequent steps and therefore affects the quality of the final sample. Common strategies are to choose the order randomly or based on local confidence estimates. t","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T14:29:29Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23531.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在“逐步填充/掩码重建”的图像生成范式里，生成顺序（先填哪里、每步填多少）会显著影响最终质量，但常见的固定/随机/基于置信度的贪心顺序要么不看全局回报，要么只看一步代理指标。论文要解决的是：能否把“选择下一步生成哪些位置、以及是否撤销早期决定并重生成”当成一个序列决策问题，用直接面向最终样本质量的训练信号学出一个样本自适应的生成顺序与“反思(rethinking)”策略，并且不依赖生成器的显式似然（以适配连续token/扩散头）。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的启发点主要在“训练目标与控制策略的解耦”以及“用分布级指标构造可用于RL的样本级奖励”。PONDER把生成器视为冻结的世界模型/条件分布近似器，把顺序与重生成当作可学习的控制器；这类结构在多模态统一建模、any-order/masked生成、以及AR+diffusion混合范式里都很常见。另一个关键贡献是FIDgrad：把FID这种分布级指标转成每个样本的边际贡献估计，从而能用策略梯度直接优化“更接近评测指标”的目标，这对“用不可微/分布级指标做训练信号”的预训练与后训练接口也有参考价值（类似把judge/偏好/分布距离变成可学习信号）。整体更偏方法论与训练信号工程，而不是纯粹的视觉下游技巧。","technical_approach":"方法由三块组成：\n1) backbone得到每个patch的特征；然后由policy决定动作a_t：选择一组要unmask并生成的patch，同时可选择一组已生成patch重新mask（rethink），使其在后续步骤被重生成。这样把“先生成哪里/是否推翻早期决定”显式化。\n2) changes final sample quality because learned conditionals are imperfect. Existing schedules are either content-agnostic (fixed/random) or rely on myopic proxies (confidence/uncertainty). The paper tackles a control question: can we learn a sample-adaptive generation ordering policy that optimizes final-sample quality directly, and can the policy also decide to undo earlier predictions (“rethink”) when later context reveals inconsistencies—without requiring access to the generator’s likelihood, so it works with continuous-token diffusion-style heads?","importance":"For foundation-model pretraining researchers, the value is less about image generation per se and more about a reusable pattern: decouple a frozen generative model from a learned controller trained with end-metric rewards. The other notable contribution is FIDgrad: a practical way to turn a distribution-level metric (FID) into per-sample rewards via a first-order marginal-effect estimate, enabling policy-gradient optimization against evaluation-aligned objectives. Exact decoding fills in only one masked position per model pass, while faster multi-token decoding schemes are lossy. Speculative decoding is the standard lossless acceleration for autoregressive models. However, it does not transfer directly to DLMs, which must choose both what to generate and where. We show that this order is the missing piece: the model's own predicti","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T18:40:42Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22543.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 diffusion language model 的无损加速问题。DLM 生成时不仅要决定填什么 token，还要决定先提交哪个 masked position；现有 speculative decoding 从 AR 模型迁移过来时，往往只把重点放在 token 草稿上，忽略了 commit order 这个更难的变量。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值在于，它揭示了 DLM 解码动态里的一个关键事实：短 speculative horizon 内，目标模型上一轮的 stale token distribution 已经能给出很可靠的 token 候选，top prediction 在 92%–100% 的位置上匹配最终提交 token；真正限制多步推测的是位置提交顺序。这对后续设计 diffusion LM 的训练目标、confidence head、位置选择策略和 serving recipe 都有启发。","technical_approach":"SPOT 使用一个 0.62M 参数的小 drafter，只预测未来的 commit order，而不是完整预测 token。它读取目标模型上一轮输出的 stale distributions，在每次假设提交一个位置后，再条件化地预测下一个位置，形成 prefix-conditional 的候选状态树。token 侧主要复用目标模型已有分布，在低置信度时保留多个高概率 token 候选。验证阶段，目标模型把当前状态和草稿状态批量前向一次，接受与目标轨迹一致的最长前缀。论文还处理了采样温度大于 0 时的精确验证问题：独立采样会让两个来自同一分布的状态也可能不相等，导致错误拒绝；SPOT 使用 per-position shared Gumbel noise，把采样条件化为确定性转移。这样可以用状态相等来验证整棵 speculative tree，同时边际分布仍等于目标模型原始采样分布。","experiments":"主要实验在 SDAR-8B 上进行。相对 Spiffy 的 calibrated draft graph，SPOT 减少 15%–35% target forwards，吞吐最高达到 1.5×；adaptive-budget 配置最高达到 2.67×。相对 draft-free decoding，SPOT 最多减少 6.8× target passes，吞吐最高达到 3.2×。结果覆盖 greedy decoding 和 T=1 sampling。论文还在 SDAR-4B、SDAR-1.7B、LLaDA-2.0-mini 上评估，并测试 drafter 迁移到 LLaDA-8B 和 Dream-7B，总共覆盖 6 个 diffusion LM。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和方法部分，重点看两点：第一，stale distribution 的 token 命中率分析，它解释了为什么 drafter 应该预测位置顺序而不是重新预测 token；第二，coupled-noise verification，它是让 tree-based speculative decoding 在 sampling 下保持精确分布的关键。预计 20–30 分钟可以读完主要思路；如果做 DLM serving 或并行解码，建议再看实验中 block width、temperature、adaptive budget 的消融。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses lossless acceleration for diffusion language models. Unlike autoregressive decoding, a DLM must decide both which token to write and which masked position to commit next. Existing speculative decoding methods tend to focus on token drafting, while the harder variable is the commit-order trajectory.","importance":"The main signal for pretraining and decoding research is the diagnosis of DLM generation dynamics. Over practical speculative horizons, the target model’s stale token distributions are already strong token proposals: their top prediction matches the eventual committed token for 92%–100% of measured positions. The bottleneck is position order. This observation can influence future DLM objectives, confidence heads, unmasking policies, and serving recipes.","technical_approach":"SPOT uses a small 0.62M-parameter drafter that predicts future commit order rather than full token content. It reads stale output distributions from the target model and, after each hypothetical commitment, predicts the next position again, building a prefix-conditional tree of candidate block states. Token candidates are mostly reused from the target’s stale distributions, with multiple high-probability choices retained when confidence is low. The target evaluates current and drafted states in one batched forward pass and accepts the longest prefix consistent with its own trajectory. For nonzero-temperature sampling, the paper introduces coupled-noise verification: the drafter and verifier share per-position Gumbel noise, making the target transition deterministic conditioned on that noise. State equality can then verify an entire speculative tree, while marginalizing over the noise preserves the original target sampling distribution.","experiments":"On SDAR-8B, SPOT uses 15%–35% fewer target forwards than Spiffy’s calibrated draft graph and reaches up to 1.5× throughput; an adaptive-budget setting reaches up to 2.67×. ro","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T01:55:26Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22820.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 protected prefix 和 editable suffix。多个 edit 按源文本中的左到右顺序排列，地址始终指向原始 source position，避免前序编辑导致后续地址漂移。关键机制有两个。第一，特殊 pointer-role token 与 localization head 配对：vocabulary head 只决定这是 replacement start、deletion end、insertion location 等角色，localization head 直接在合法源位置上打分。第二，dual-index RoPE 同时编码 token 在因果流中的位置和它引用的源地址，使后续 token 可以条件化在已经生成的 pointer 上，同时仍使用标准 causal attention mask。训练上，论文把普通 LM loss 与 This makes trust binary: corrupted context is copied as ground truth, propagating errors into generated spans. We reopen this design space by combining absorbing and uniform-mixing kernels with region-dependent rates. Missing positions follow absorbing dynamics, while context undergoes low-rate random substitution, ex","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-02T15:14:44Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22380.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"离散扩散语言模型几乎都采用absorbing forward process（token→[MASK]并吸收），导致“条件上下文”在整个反向轨迹中被当作绝对真值：一旦上下文被检索/转写/代码等噪声污染，模型会把错误上下文当作100%可信并复制传播。论文要解决的是：如何让离散扩散在条件生成/infilling时具备“可疑度”（graded trust），既能在干净上下文下不退化，又能在上下文被污染时检测、修复或至少抑制错误扩散。","importance":"它把离散扩散LM里长期被忽略的“forward dynamics 设计面”重新打开，提出region-inhomogeneous forward process：对hole与context使用不同的腐蚀核与速率。对做diffusion/any-order LM预训练的人，这属于训练目标与采样过程的关键接口改动：不仅影响条件建模，也影响模型是否能对输入上下文给出校准的token-level置信度。更现实的意义在于RAG/代码补全/噪声转写等场景：上下文并不总是可信，而absorbing过程在结构上不允许模型‘回头修上下文’。","technical_approach":"核心思路分训练与采样两块。\n\n1) Region-inhomogeneous forward process（训练侧）\n- 标准masked diffusion：所有位置按absorbing核以时间t被mask，已观测上下文在所有t都保持不变。\n- 这里把序列分成hole与user-supplied context两块：\n  - hole位置：仍走标准absorbing dynamics（逐步mask）。\n  - context位置：以很小的速率η走uniform-mixing corruption（把token随机替换为词表均匀分布的token），等价于让context处在uniform-mixing的一个固定“小噪声时间”。\n- 训练目标仍是masked diffusion常用的加权交叉熵/denoising目标，只需要用新的forward边缘分布（marginals）做轻量改动。\n- 额外加入η=0的mixture分量，确保模型仍覆盖“完全干净条件”的训练分布，避免在clean conditioning上退化。\n\n2) Detect–remask–refill sampler with trust annealing（推理侧）\n- 由于context也被轻微corrupt训练过，模型在每个位置都会输出“该位置观测token的概率”，从而得到一个免费suspicion score（模型自己觉得这个上下文token像不像真的）。\n- 采样时在反向早期：对低置信的context token执行re-noise（把它们重新mask/吸收到[MASK]），再用常规反向动态在双向条件下refill；随着时间推进到lock time后冻结context，不再改动。\n- 论文还讨论了与low-confidence decoding的组合需要的置信度记账（bookkeeping）。\n\n作者把absorbing masked diffusion解释为“trust轴”的一端：已知区SNR无限大；他们的方法相当于把context从无限SNR拉回到有限SNR，从而允许‘怀疑—修复’。","experiments":"实验围绕“被污染上下文的infilling”展开，并用预注册假设与decision gate区分训练改动与采样改动。\n- 现象诊断：在absorbing设定下，即使给模型喂uniform-random garbage上下文，预训练模型对这些观测token仍会给出很高的复制倾向（文中提到对观测context token分配约50%概率的量级），说明其对context缺乏可疑度表达。\n- 九个实验arm拆分：仅finetune forward过程、仅trust-annealing采样、两者结合、以及包含η=0 mixture等。\n- 结果：\n  - 对“显著/均匀”的上下文腐蚀（conspicuous uniform corruptions），finetune后模型能更好地检测并修复/隔离污染，且在干净上下文下没有可测回归。\n  - 对“更像真实世界”的腐蚀（plausible corruptions），检测不完全、infilling收益小，预注册的主F1 gate未通过。\n- 消融局限：作者承认尚未完全分离η=0 mixture与trust annealing等因素的独立贡献。\n- 解释性观察（非严格结论）：可检测性与损害程度似乎与“腐蚀核与真实数据条件分布的散度”相关。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约45–60分钟）：\n- 先读引言：它把absorbing过程的问题用“binary trust / infinite SNR”讲得很清楚，适合快速建立直觉。\n- 重点看Section 3：region-inhomogeneous forward process的形式化与训练目标如何改；这是可复现实现的关键。\n- 再看Section 3.4：detect–remask–refill与trust annealing的具体流程与lock time机制，理解它如何在不破坏条件生成的前提下允许‘修上下文’。\n- 实验部分优先看九臂对照与预注册gate：哪些收益来自训练、哪些来自采样，作者自己也给了负结果与不确定性边界。\n- 如果要复现/迁移到RAG或代码场景，建议额外关注：η的选择、η=0 mixture比例、以及“plausible corruption”失败时的错误模式。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Discrete diffusion LMs for infilling largely default to the absorbing forward process (tokens jump to [MASK] and stay). This makes conditioning context effectively noise-free for the entire reverse trajectory—i.e., the model must trust the context absolutely. When the provided context is corrupted (retrieval errors, buggy code, noisy transcripts), the model copies errors and cannot revise them. token-level sparse attention during long-context autoregressive decoding. Sparse attention reduces compute by attending to a small set of past tokens, but finding the top-k tokens at every layer still requires full-context scoring over the KV cache. Static cross-layer index sharing reduces this overhead by computing indices only at anchor layers, but it assumes that attention patterns remain stable across depth. In long contexts, the reused indices drift away from the layer-specific optimum and cause attention loss.","importance":"This is primarily an inference-efficiency paper, but it is relevant to long-context foundation model research. It clarifies that the bottleneck of token-level sparsity is not only the sparse attention kernel; sparse index construction can dominate when the context is long. The key empirical observation is also useful: salient-token shifts across layers are mostly local rather than arbitrary. This can inform inference systems, sparse-attention architectures, and possibly pretraining choices for long-context models. The reported system-level gain is substantial: at 128K context length, Prism Attention achieves a 3.4x end-to-end decoding speedup over FlashAttention while maintaining long-context task quality.","technical_approach":"Prism Attention replaces rigid index reuse with a proposal-refinement mechanism. At selected proposal layers, it builds a relaxed candidate set rather than a narrow top-k set. Subsequent refined layers then perform lightweight token selection within this expanded candidate pool. This design follows the paper’s attention-dynamics analysis: the core high-attention tokens tend to remain stable across adjacent layers, while newly important tokens are mostly near the boundary of the previous selection instead of being scattered across the full context. The method has two main components. Dynamic Index Refinement adapts the sparse indices within the proposal pool to track local layer-wise drift. Quantized Proposal Generation reduces the cost of full-context proposal construction using low-bit scoring. Compared with block-sparse methods, it keeps token-level selectivity. Compared with static cross-layer sharing, it explicitly handles depth-wise attention drift through a coarse-to-fine selection process.","experiments":"The paper evaluates on reasoning and long-context benchmarks against mainstream sparse-attention baselines. The headline number is a 3.4x end-to-end decoding speedup over FlashAttention at 128K context length. The diagnostic experiments show that the overlap between anchor-layer top-k indices and later-layer top-k indices decays as layer distance increases, and the decay is stronger at 8K context than at 1K. At the same time, most newly selected salient tokens are captured by an expanded proposal region. This supports the central claim: cross-layer reuse is viable if the reused set is not too rigid. The most important ablations are proposal size, refinement frequency, quantized proposal accuracy, and the quality-speed tradeoff across context lengths.","reading_guide":"Read carefully. Start with Figure 1 and Figure 2, since they motivate the whole method: Figure 1 shows overlap decay under static sharing, and Figure 2 shows that the shift of salient tokens is localized. In the experiments, prioritize the 128K end-to-end speed results, long-context quality retention, and ablations for proposal size, refinement, and quantization. Existing inference-time visual token pruning methods often irreversibly remove low-ranked tokens, although the set of visual tokens receiving high query-conditioned attention can change across decoder depth. Recoverable routing preserves skipped tokens for later reconsideration, but re-admits them with stale representations after bypassing i","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T11:28:46Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23324.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 LVLM 推理阶段的一个具体但常见的问题：视觉 token 剪枝可以省算力，但早期被剪掉的 token 可能在更深层才变得与当前文本查询相关；如果允许它们后续回流，又会遇到另一个问题——这些 token 跳过了中间若干层，隐藏状态停留在旧深度，和当前层的活跃 token 不在同一个表示空间。论文把这两个问题拆开来看：一是“相关性随深度变化”，二是“回流 token 的表示过时”。核心目标不是提出新的训练范式，而是在不改模型参数、不重新训练的前提下，让可恢复的视觉 token 路由真正可用。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接影响有限，因为它主要是推理优化工作，不是训练 recipe 或建模目标创新。但它对原生多模态模型有两个启发。第一，视觉证据的查询相关性在 decoder 深度上并不稳定，说明把视觉 token 重要性视为静态量是有问题的，这对训练期的 token routing、层间压缩、甚至多模态 MoE 设计都有参考价值。第二，论文明确指出“可恢复路由”真正难的不是把 token 存起来，而是如何补偿它错过的表示演化；这相当于把层间状态迁移当成一个近似问题来处理。若以后做训练期动态 token 计算、跨层跳连压缩、或 unified multimodal token budget 分配，这个视角是有用的。","technical_approach":"方法叫 CatchUp，核心是 depth-aware re-admission。它不是一次性决定保留哪些视觉 token，而是在若干选定 decoder 层重新做 query-conditioned relevance 评估。具体做法分三步。第一步，在路由层用最后一个文本 token 到各视觉 token 的注意力作为相关性代理，选出当前要激活的视觉 token，其余 token 不删除，而是 deferred。第二步，在两个路由层之间，只让被选中的视觉 token 继续经过后续 decoder 计算，从而减少 attention、FFN 和 KV cache 开销。第三步，当某个 deferred token 在更深层再次被选中时，不直接拿它旧的 hidden state 回来，而是做一次“catch-up”更新，近似它在被跳过层中本该经历的表示变化。\n\n论文的关键创新在第三步。作者先用 full-token reference trajectory 做分析，发现如果把回流 token 的 stale state 替换成同深度 oracle state，性能会明显恢复，这证明问题确实出在表示失配。于是 CatchUp 用“reference-to-current transition signals”来近似这个缺失的演化。直观上，它会从语义和空间上相近的活跃视觉 token 中提取从参考深度到当前深度的状态变化，再把这种变化迁移到回流 token 上。也就是说，它不是重新计算被跳过的所有层，而是借邻近 token 的演化轨迹做插值式补偿。论文强调这是 training-free 的，因此它和需要额外蒸馏或轻量适配器训练的方法不同。\n\n与已有方法相比，区别很清楚。相对 irreversible pruning，它允许晚出现的重要视觉证据重新进入计算图；相对 recoverable routing 但不做状态修正的方法，它解决了 re-admission 后 hidden state 过时的问题。作者还专门用 oracle 实验说明：如果只允许回流但不修正状态，收益有限；如果状态能对齐到当前深度，性能会显著更好，说明 CatchUp 的设计点是对的。","experiments":"实验主要在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 上做，重点覆盖 referring-expression grounding 和通用多模态基准。动机实验先给出两个很有信息量的统计。其一，在 RefCOCO 的 500 个样本、576 个视觉 token、top-64 相关 token 的设定下，相邻 decoder 层之间的 top-K token 集合平均波动率达到 28.85%，说明相关证据在层间变化很快。其二，以第 2 层作为早期选择参考，late-emerging evidence ratio 在第 7 层就超过 50%，到第 31 层达到 99.42%，也就是后期最相关的 token 几乎都不在早期 top-K 里。这直接否定了“早剪枝后一路沿用”的静态假设。\n\n主结果上，CatchUp 在相同 routing budget 下取得了被比较方法里最好的 grounding 保留率；在 matched prefill computation 的设置下，它使用最少的视觉 token 和最小的 decoder KV-cache，同时相对 dense inference 带来 8.0% 的端到端时延下降。论文摘要和正文都强调它在两个 benchmark group 上平均 retention 最高。虽然给出的公开片段里没有完整表格数值，但结论很明确：它不是单纯追求更激进压缩，而是在相同预算下把精度损失压得更低。\n\n消融方面，最值得注意的是 oracle state replacement。作者把回流 token 的 stale state 直接替换成 full-token 轨迹中同层的真实状态，grounding 性能显著提升，证明“状态陈旧”是 recoverable routing 的主要瓶颈，而不是回流机制本身无效。另一个关键消融是不同 catch-up 信号来源：语义邻近和空间邻近是互补的，只靠其中一种通常不如联合使用。还有路由层位置和预算的实验，显示过早、过少的路由决策会错过后期证据，而过于频繁的重评估又会侵蚀效率收益，说明该方法依赖一个比较平衡的 routing schedule。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐式推导。建议先看 Introduction 和 Motivating Observations，重点看 Figure 1 和 Figure 2。这里已经把论文最重要的经验事实讲清楚：视觉 token 的相关性是深度相关的，而且 stale state 会伤性能。然后看方法部分里关于 deferred token re-admission 和 transition signal 的定义，弄清楚 CatchUp 不是重新跑缺失层，而是借相邻 token 的状态变化做近似补偿。实验部分优先看三类内容：一是 matched budget 下和 irreversible / recoverable baselines 的对比；二是 oracle replacement 消融；三是 latency、KV-cache、active token 数量这些系统指标。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主要信息。如果只关心对多模态预训练的启发，重点记住两点：token relevance 随层变化，以及层间表示演化可以被近似建模。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This paper studies a specific failure mode in efficient LVLM inference. Visual token pruning saves compute, but tokens dropped early may become relevant only at later decoder depths. Recoverable routing tries to bring them back, yet the returned tokens carry stale hidden states because they skipped intermediate decoder transformations. The paper isolates these as two separate issues: relevance is depth-dependent, and re-admitted tokens are representation-misaligned. The goal is to make recoverable visual-token routing actually work without retraining the model.","importance":"The direct impact on pretraining is limited since this is primarily an inference-efficiency paper, not a new training objective or recipe. Still, it offers two useful lessons for foundation-model research. First, visual token importance is not stable across decoder depth, which matters for training-time token routing, layerwise compression, and multimodal compute allocation. Second, the hard part of recoverable routing is not preserving skipped tokens but compensating for the representation evolution they missed. That framing is relevant to any future work on dynamic token computation, cross-layer skipping, or multimodal budgeted processing.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. We present FUTRANSFORMER, a CUDA implementation of a Llama-style Transformer block for NVIDIA Hopper GPUs. The paper aims to deliver a Hopper-optimized CUDA implementation for Llama-style blocks that uses FP8 for prefill and INT4 for decode, while aggressively fusing non-GEMM but bandwidth-heavy operations (RoPE, SwiGLU, RMSNorm, residuals, KV writes) into GEMM epilogues to avoid extra HBM round-trips.","importance":"Its impact on pretraining is indirect but practical:\n1) Many pretraining-adjacent workflows (synthetic data generation, verifier/teacher evaluation, long-context regression tests) are inference-heavy; separating and optimizing prefill vs decode changes the cost profile of the whole train–eval–data loop.\n2) The paper is a concrete demonstration of where inference bottlenecks are structural (memory traffic, layout, intermediate tensors). a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T03:03:28Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22858.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的问题是：在用 Beam/MCTS 等树搜索做 test-time scaling 时，为什么“每一步固定扩展预算”会浪费大量算力，以及如何在不训练额外模型的前提下，把扩展预算动态分配到真正不确定、值得探索的推理步骤上，从而降低 token/延迟成本而不明显掉准确率。","importance":"这篇更偏推理时效率与搜索策略，不是预训练 recipe。但它对基座研究仍有两点启发：\n1) 训练信号与推理轨迹的冗余：作者观察到同一中间状态采样出的后续大量重复，提示“模型在某些状态下的分布有效支持很小”，这类现象也会反过来影响用搜索轨迹做数据合成（post-train）时的多样性与去重策略。\n2) 不确定性代理的工程化：用 sibling action 的语义离散度（cosine dispersion）作为不确定性 proxy，并据此决定 Monte Carlo 采样预算，提供了一个训练无关、可迁移的分配思路，适合接到各种树搜索框架里。\n总体属于 skim：方法清晰、工程可用，但对预训练核心问题（数据/优化/稳定性）是间接相关。","technical_approach":"提出 Uncertainty-Aware Allocation (UAA)，核心是把“选谁扩展”和“扩展多少”解耦：\n- value 决定哪些候选 action 值得保留；\n- uncertainty 决定每个节点/子树要分配多少扩展预算。\n\n关键观察：从同一中间状态采样的 continuations 大量语义重复，最终答案会塌缩到少数几类；并且当前步 sibling actions 的语义离散度可以预测其下游 continuations 的冗余程度，因此可以在真正展开子树前就设定预算。\n\n方法组件：\n- 不确定性度量：用 sibling actions 的 embedding cosine 相似度/离散度作为轻量 proxy（无需训练额外模型）。离散度低 → 不确定性低 → 下游更冗余。\n- 信息论动机：把每步预算视为 Monte Carlo 样本量，用于估计该步“剩余答案熵（residual answer entropy）”。在 Lipschitz 假设下，给出一个 sufficient budget：当不确定性低（离散度低）时，为达到固定精度所需样本量更小。\n- 两个机制：\n  1) UAP (uncertainty-aware pruning)：低不确定性步只保留高质量子集。\n  2) UAB (uncertainty-aware budgeting)：对保留的低不确定性 action，缩小下一步扩展预算。\n- 适配性：宣称可 plug-and-play 接到 Beam Search、MCTS、DVTS、REBASE 等不同搜索算法；并在白盒（可取 embedding/logprob）与黑盒（如 GPT-4o）场景下用不同不确定性 proxy。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段可确认的结果点：\n- 现象验证：在多种任务（不仅是数学，还包括 StrategyQA、planning）与不同算法/模型（如 可能承担了把任务约束传到答案位置的主要作用。这对后续做训练时的路由正则、模板设计、推理时 selective full-expert 保留，都有启发。贡献量级属于中等偏上，主要是细致的干预分析和较克制的机制归因，而不是提出新的压缩算法。","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"实验主任务是 HotpotQA distractor setting，prompt 大约 1,800 tokens，要求模型只输出 short answer，用 greedy decoding 评 F1。主模型 Qwen3-30B-A3B（关闭 reasoning），另外还测了 DeepSeek-V2-Lite-Chat、Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B-Chat、OLMoE-1B-7B-Instruct。Qwen3 上，2,000 题设置里原模型 F1 为 69.81；uniform 8→4 在不同 batch 上大约带来 -3.34 到 -3.38 F1 的损失。作者把这部分损失拆开后发现，主要不是 recall 掉很多，而是答案长度和 precision 受影响，符合“知道答案但没按短答格式说”的判断。\n\n定位实验里，单 token 干预用 500 题，span / family 对比用 2,000 题。Qwen3 中仅恢复答案前两个 token 的 full experts，就能解释大约一半的 answer likelihood 损失；DeepSeek-V2-Lite 中 prompt 最前面 8–32 个 token 约能解释五分之一左右的损失。作者也强调：按 answer NLL 看，这些位置只能解释“部分”损失，说明还有更分散的次级影响，但 F1 的主要下降已经高度局部化。\n\n在 equal-budget 的真实 prompt 测试里，作者比较了把有限 full-expert 预算放在不同位置的效果。结论是，把预算放在 task interface 上明显比随机撒在文档段落里更有用；在他们的模板里，“保留两端”这类简单规则和更复杂的 marks/selector 没拉开明显差距，因此更像是一个 placement principle：优先保留 prompt 接口和答案邻近位置，而不是提出了新的最优选择器。实验设计上还有一点值得注意：部分 batch 的 primary comparison 和 decision rule 是预先写进脚本的，作者明确区分 prespecified 和 exploratory/post hoc，这让结果可信度比常见纯探索性分析高一些。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但如果你做 MoE serving，很值得把图表看细。先看 Section 3.1，理解作者为什么先把 F1 拆成 precision / recall / answer length；这是全文最重要的判断基础。然后看 Section 3.2–3.3 的 token / span intervention 图，重点找“少数 token 决定大部分损失”的证据，以及不同模型家族关键位置为何不同。接着看 Section 4，尤其是 path blocking、layer repair 和对 attention sink / super expert / massive activation 的排除性测量。这里的价值不在于给出唯一机制，而在于把几个流行解释逐一压缩到较小范围。最后看 Section 5 的 equal-budget placement 实验，它直接告诉你工程上 full-expert 预算该优先放哪里。\n\n如果时间有限，30 分钟内看完 Section 3 和 Section 5 就够抓住主结论；若你关心训练侧如何让 MoE 对 prefill reduction 更稳，再补 Section 4 和附录里的 batch 来源、统计规则、vLLM 复现实验细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a practical MoE serving question: if prompt tokens use fewer routed experts during prefill, what exactly breaks? Instead of stopping at an average quality drop, the authors localize the damage by token, span, layer, and attention path. The main finding is that on short-answer QA, halving prefill experts in three of four MoE families mostly hurts answer form rather than answer finding: the model often still knows the answer, but fails to answer briefly. Moreover, much of that loss is concentrated in a very small number of prompt tokens.","importance":"This is an inference-efficiency paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it turns a serving trade-off into a mechanistic question. Two points matter. First, prompt tokens are not equally sensitive to expert reduction; a small set of interface tokens can dominate the quality loss. Second, the results suggest a link between MoE routing capacity and instruction transport: some tokens appear to carry task-format constraints to the answer position. That has implications for selective full-expert serving, prompt/template design, and possibly training-time routing regularization. The contribution is mainly in careful intervention-based analysis, not in a new compression method.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"Read Section 3.1 first. The F1 decomposition into precision, recall, and answer length is the foundation for the whole argument. Then read Sections 3.2–3.3 carefully for the token and span interventions; that is where the paper earns its main claim that a few prompt tokens carry most of the loss. After that, go to Section 4 for the path-blocking and layer-repair analysis, plus the direct measurements that rule out attention-sink and super-expert explanations as the main story. Finish with Section 5, which is the most actionable engineering section because it tests where a limited full-expert budget should be placed.\n\nIf you only have half an hour, Sections 3 and 5 are enough to capture the main result. If you care about how training might make MoE models more robust to prefill reduction, then Section 4 and the appendices on batch provenance, statistical rules, and vLLM reproduction details are worth the extra time.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看敏感 token 定位和恢复实验即可；若内部做 MoE prefill 降本，应立刻复现这种 per-token full-expert whitelist。","en":"Read the sensitive-token localization and recovery experiments; if you serve MoEs, reproduce the per-token full-expert whitelist internally."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zhiyuan Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["moe-serving","prefill","expert-routing","token-sensitivity","inference-optimization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22454","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.22454","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.23455","title":"HydraLLM: Adaptive Width Slicing for Elastic Text and Vision-Language Models","abstract":"LLMs and LVLMs are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware, ranging from data-center GPUs to resource-constrained edge devices. Their fixed-size architectures limit the ability to reuse a single model across deployment settings with different compute, latency, and model-size targets. We introduce HydraLLM, an elastic MQA-based architecture. Its number of active attention heads determines the effective widths of the embeddings, attention projections, feed-forward layers, and the outpu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T13:38:11Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23455.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是：如何把一个已经训练好的静态 decoder-only LLM/LVLM，改造成一个可按运行时预算连续裁宽的单检查点模型，同时尽量保住小宽度子网的质量。难点不在于单纯做结构剪枝，而在于 prefix slicing 这种“只保留前 k 个通道/头”的约束很强：如果原始权重里重要单元没有排到前面，小子网会直接丢掉关键信息，性能会很差。作者针对这个问题提出了从静态模型到弹性模型的转换流程，目标是在不重训整族模型的前提下，让同一套参数支持多种宽度预算，并且把这种机制从纯文本扩展到视觉-语言模型。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在部署侧和训练接口侧，而不是新的预训练范式。它提供了一个比较务实的思路：先做标准预训练，再通过后续的重排+弹性适配训练，把静态模型变成可切片模型。这个方向对“一个 checkpoint 服务多档算力”的场景有直接价值，也对模型压缩、蒸馏替代方案、以及 post-pretrain 阶段的结构再参数化有启发。贡献量级上，它更像一套工程上可落地的 elastic conversion recipe，而不是改变语言建模本身的工作。对预训练研究员来说，最有价值的点是：作者证明了 head-aligned width slicing 在 decoder-only MQA 架构里是可行的，而且小子网质量高度依赖重要性重排与子网采样训练这两个步骤。","technical_approach":"方法核心有三部分。第一，结构设计。HydraLLM 采用 MQA 架构，把“激活多少个 query heads”作为唯一的运行时宽度控制变量 k。embedding 维度、attention projection、FFN hidden width、输出头维度都按 k/Hq 线性缩放，并且始终取前缀通道，因此天然形成严格嵌套的子网族。之所以选 MQA，而不是标准 MHA，是因为共享 KV 头后，按 query head 做宽度切片更整齐，跨宽度共享也更简单。\n\n第二，静态到弹性的离线重排。作者指出，预训练好的静态模型并不满足“前缀越靠前越重要”的条件，所以直接切前缀会伤得很重。为此他们先在一个校准语料上收集激活统计，再按单元重要性对各个可切片维度做 permutation，把更重要的 embedding channels、attention units、FFN neurons、LM head channels 移到前面。文中比较了三种重要性指标：activation magnitude、VxC、CETT。论文给出的结论是 CETT 更适合 prefix slicing，因为它更直接衡量单元对组件输出的归一化贡献，而不是只看激活大小。重排时不是局部随便换，而是要把 embedding、输入输出投影、FFN、输出头等相关张量同步置换，保证函数语义一致。\n\n第三，stochastic subnetwork training。完成重排后，作者不同时训练所有宽度，而是在每一步随机采样一个 k，只更新对应激活子网。由于前缀单元会在更多 k 下被激活，它们天然获得更多梯度，重要性排序会被进一步强化。这和 once-for-all / nested network 的训练逻辑相近，但这里是从已有 LLM checkpoint 出发做适配，而不是从头训练。训练目标上仍是标准语言建模损失，没有引入复杂蒸馏或多目标约束。对 LVLM，作者额外设计了 elastic projection module，把视觉编码器特征映射到不同宽度的文本 embedding 空间，使视觉侧也能跟随文本子网一起裁宽。","experiments":"实验重点看三个层面。第一，小宽度文本子网是否还能用。论文在 TriviaQA 上报告：当只激活 75% heads 时，HydraLLM 比一个参数量还多 17.25% 的基线配置高 2.2 个百分点，说明重排+适配训练后，子网并不是简单的劣化版裁剪模型。第二，极小宽度下重排是否关键。作者给出 25% 配置下 importance-based reordering 可把 post-training perplexity 改善 16.2%，这基本说明如果没有离线重排，prefix slicing 很难成立。第三，推理效率收益。25% 宽度时 token generation throughput 相比全模型提升 75.29%，说明这种结构裁宽确实能换来实际吞吐，而不只是参数计数上的缩减。\n\n多模态部分，作者把同样的 slicing 原则扩展到 LVLM，并报告 50% heads 激活时 CLIPScore 达到 66.9，高于 Gemma 3 4B 的 65.3，且后者规模约为其 7 倍。这个结果说明弹性投影模块没有把视觉-文本对齐完全破坏掉。不过从基座研究角度，CLIPScore 只能说明生成质量的一部分，最好结合更多 VLM 指标看稳健性。\n\n消融上，最该关注的是：不同重要性指标的比较、是否做重排、不同宽度采样策略、以及文本模型到多模态模型的迁移效果。论文传达出的主要经验是，小子网质量主要由“重排质量”和“适配训练是否覆盖各宽度”决定，而不是单靠结构设计本身。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看方法图和转换流程，再看重排与消融。优先读 Introduction、Section 3.1 到 3.3，以及实验里关于 25%/50%/75% 宽度的主表。重点问题有三个：\n1. 为什么 MQA 是这个方案成立的前提之一；\n2. CETT 相比 activation magnitude / VxC 到底好在哪里，是否只是经验上更稳；\n3. 子网训练时的宽度采样分布会不会偏向大网或小网，从而影响不同预算下的公平性。\n\n如果时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够抓住主线。值得深挖的点是重排传播规则：embedding 通道、attention 投影、FFN 和输出头之间如何保持一致置换；以及这种方法能否迁移到 GQA、标准 MHA、MoE 或更强的 VLM 主干。另一个值得追问的问题是，这种后处理式弹性化在更大规模预训练模型上是否仍稳定，还是会受到层间耦合和 tokenizer/rope 设计的限制。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical but nontrivial problem: how to turn a pretrained static decoder-only LLM or LVLM into a single elastic checkpoint that can run at multiple width budgets, while keeping the smaller subnetworks useful. The difficulty is not generic pruning. Their slicing rule is strict prefix slicing: keep the first k heads/channels/neurons and drop the rest. A standard pretrained checkpoint is not organized that way, so naive slicing removes important units and collapses small-width quality. The work proposes a static-to-elastic conversion pipeline that makes prefix-based width control viable for both text-only and vision-language models.","importance":"This is more relevant to deployment-aware model design than to core language modeling objectives, but it is still useful for foundation-model researchers. The main value is the recipe: pretrain once in the usual way, then convert the model into an elastic family of nested subnetworks through reordering and adaptation training. That matters for serving across heterogeneous hardware, for post-pretraining structural adaptation, and as an alternative to training many separate compressed models. The contribution is not a new pretraining paradigm. The useful takeaway is narrower and concrete: head-aligned width slicing can work in decoder-only MQA models, and the quality of small subnetworks depends heavily on importance-aware reordering plus stochastic subnetwork training.","technical_approach":"$2e","experiments":"$2f","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the architecture figure and the static-to-elastic pipeline, then move to the reordering section and the main ablations. The most important parts are the introduction, Sections 3.1 to 3.3, and the tables covering 25%, 50%, and 75% width settings. Focus on three questions: why MQA is structurally convenient here; why CETT works better than activation magnitude or VxC; and how the width-sampling schedule may bias optimization toward larger or smaller subnetworks.\n\nA 30-40 minute skim is enough to capture the main idea. If you want to go deeper, inspect how permutations are propagated across embeddings, attention projections, FFN layers, and the LM head, and ask whether the same recipe would transfer to GQA, standard MHA, MoE, or larger multimodal backbones. Another useful question is whether this post-hoc elasticization remains stable at larger scales, where layer coupling and positional/tokenization design may matter more.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将 MQA 机制与弹性网络（Elastic Network）结合，通过控制活跃 head 数量来动态调整模型宽度，为端侧部署提供了一种灵活的 once-for-all 方案，值得关注其静态到弹性的转换 pipeline。","en":"Combines MQA with elastic networks by controlling active heads to dynamically adjust model width, offering a flexible once-for-all solution for edge deployment; the static-to-elastic conversion pipeline is worth skimming."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Chalmers University of Technology"],"authors":["Oscar Kemme","Janek Haberer","Ali Hojjat","Olaf Landsiedel"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Elastic Architecture","Width Slicing","MQA","Knowledge Distillation","Model Compression"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23455","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.23455","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.23613","title":"Trajectory-Triggered Sparse Collaborative Decoding","abstract":"Dense assistance methods such as speculative decoding repeatedly verify draft tokens with a target model, keeping target computation coupled to generation. We study sparse collaborative decoding, where a draft model generates most tokens and a target model is invoked only for short corrective segments. The key problem is deciding when to call the target. We show that local uncertainty is a weak trigger: entropy, negative log-likelihood, logit margin, top-$k$ mass, and velocity features give near","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T15:24:05Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23613.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是小模型与大模型协同推理中的一个具体决策问题：在生成过程内部，什么时候值得调用一次更强的 target model 来做短段纠偏，而不是一开始就全程用大模型，或像 speculative decoding 那样持续验证。作者关心的不是如何让 target 更频繁介入，而是如何在尽量少调用 target 的前提下，把调用放在“足够早、又足够有把握”的位置。论文的核心发现是，单步局部不确定性几乎不能预测最终推理是否会失败；真正有用的是整段置信轨迹的形状特征。","importance":"这不是预训练论文，和基座训练的直接关系有限，更接近推理时的小大模型协作策略。但它对 serving 和 inference-time scaling 有启发，尤其适合关注 draft-target 协同、黑盒 API 协作、以及 reasoning 轨迹控制的人。贡献不在新模型结构，而在把“何时介入”从经验规则变成了一个可学习的轨迹级触发问题。对基座研究员的间接价值有两点：一是说明 token-level entropy 这类常用不确定性信号在多步推理里很弱，不能想当然地拿来做路由；二是提示如果未来要把小模型作为大模型的前端代理，训练时可能需要显式塑造可预测的 trajectory signals，而不是只优化最终答案。","technical_approach":"方法框架叫 sparse collaborative decoding。设一个 draft model 负责生成大部分 token，一个更强的 target model 只在触发时生成一个有长度上限的 corrective segment，然后 draft 再接着往下写。整个干预完全是文本级的：target 生成的片段被直接拼到上下文里，不共享 logits、hidden states 或参数，因此兼容黑盒 target，也区别于 speculative decoding 那种逐段验证机制。\n\n论文的关键在 trigger policy。作者先系统检验了直觉上最自然的一类信号：当前 token 的 entropy、negative log-likelihood、logit margin、top-k mass，以及短窗口 velocity 特征。结果这些局部信号对“最终会不会答错”的预测几乎接近随机。原因也很合理：推理模型可能在错误路径上非常自信，也可能在正确路径上短暂犹豫。\n\n于是作者改做 trajectory-level failure prediction。具体做法是，不看单点，而是对 draft 到当前时刻为止的置信轨迹做聚合统计。特征来自多种基础信号的时间序列，包括 entropy、NLL、margin、概率集中度及其时间变化；再提取均值、极值、分位数、斜率、方差、位置相关行为等 summary features。然后用一个很轻量的 logistic predictor，仅根据 draft 自身的正确/错误标签来训练失败预测器。在线推理时，随着 draft 逐步生成，系统不断更新这些轨迹特征；当预测失败风险超过阈值，就调用 target 生成一个短纠偏段。\n\n这个设计带来一个明确的 trigger-timing tradeoff：越早调用，target 越有机会改变后续轨迹，但证据更弱；越晚调用，失败预测更准，但可纠偏空间更小。论文把这个权衡显式化，并与 open-loop 固定位置调用、整题级 cascade routing、posthoc rewriting、self-consistency、speculative decoding 等策略做比较。","experiments":"最关键的实验结论有三条。第一，局部不确定性几乎没法用。作者在 GSM8K 上用 Qwen-1.5B 作为 draft 时，单步 entropy 和 NLL 的 failure prediction AUC 只有 0.531 和 0.528，velocity 特征也基本无效，说明常见的 token-level confidence 不能胜任触发器。第二，轨迹聚合特征明显更强。轻量 logistic predictor 的 AUC 达到 0.86，这个提升幅度足够说明“轨迹形状”比“当前 token 状态”更接近真正的失败信号。第三，把这个预测器用于在线触发后，效果优于固定调度的 open-loop SCD：在五组 GSM8K draft-target 配对上，相比最佳 open-loop schedule，准确率提升 5.3 到 20.0 个点。\n\n论文还报告了向 StrategyQA 的迁移，并在 MATH-500 上观察到改进，但作者也强调一个前提：early prediction 和 target competence 必须匹配。也就是说，触发器即使能较早发现风险，如果 target 对该类错误并不擅长，收益也有限。评估指标除了最终准确率，还包括 target calls、target-generated tokens、wall-clock time、rescue rate 和 break rate。这些指标很重要，因为它们能区分“多花 target 预算换来提升”和“真正高效地把 target 用在该用的位置”。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓住两个表：一个是局部不确定性特征的 AUC，另一个是 trajectory features 的 AUC 和在线触发结果。优先读 Introduction、Problem Setup、Trajectory-Level Failure Prediction，以及主结果表。阅读重点不是模型细节，而是作者如何定义可观测状态、如何构造轨迹统计特征、以及如何分析 trigger timing 的取舍。\n\n如果只想判断这篇论文是否值得继续跟，可以用 25 到 35 分钟读完主线。值得细看的地方有三处：\n1.
