predictivity高）并不能区分两种几何结构——(1) 真正的“线性世界模型”：表示的大部分方差由这些因子主导；(2) 类核机器/随机特征：因子确实线性可读出，但只占表示方差的一小部分，表示主体是高维弯曲流形。论文要解决的是：给出可操作的诊断方法，判断一个encoder是否真的接近“线性世界模型”，而不是仅仅“足够富的特征映射”。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值在评估与机制分析方法论：很多关于“表示是否对齐某些潜变量/语义因子”的结论依赖线性probe，进而影响对数据、目标函数（对比学习/自监督/多模态对齐）、以及模型可解释性的判断。论文给出一套不对称诊断（不仅看z能否从f(x)读出，还看f(x)能否从z反推、方差谱是否集中、以及跨分布/跨风格的泛化），能更稳地回答“模型是否把因子放在表示的主子空间里”。这对做预训练表征分析、选择中间层特征、以及解释OOD鲁棒性差异都有帮助。","technical_approach":"核心定义与诊断：\n- 定义1（linear world model）：对输入x的潜变量z(x)做白化后，特征f(x)可以写成f = W z~ + ε，其中ε是与z无关/非线性的残差；并且用σ^2刻画残差相对潜变量对齐方差的比例。关键点是“潜变量对齐方向应当主导表示方差”，这比“存在一个线性读出器”强得多。\n- 理论动机：基于随机特征/核方法理论，若特征维度足够大，几乎任何平滑目标都能被线性读出；因此forward probe高R^2并不证明表示几何是线性的。\n- 三个不对称诊断（用于区分Def.1与核机器式特征）：\n  1) Reverse predictivity：不仅训练z←f(x)的线性probe，也看f(x)←z的线性回归能解释多少方差；若z只嵌在高维弯曲流形里，反向很差。\n  2) Spectral concentration：检查与z对齐的信号是否集中在前若干主成分（PCs）里；线性世界模型应在谱上更集中。\n  3) OOD generalization：在跨风格/跨分布设置下测试上述关系是否保持；核机器式特征更可能依赖分布细节。\n- 为了让reverse predictivity可比，论文还提出一个“ceiling”来校准不同模型/不同维度下反向可达到的上限（细节在方法章节与附录）。\n实验设计上，先用完全可控的模拟（2D潜变量→随机Fourier特征→随机/训练MLP）验证诊断能区分“随机特征可线性读出”与“训练后接近Def.1”的两种极端；再用SVG-World构造跨渲染风格但共享潜变量布局的测试床，评估真实预训练ViT的处于哪一侧。","experiments":"论文报告的关键经验结论包括：\n- linearly accessible directions. The paper argues this inference is too permissive: high forward linear decodability can arise both when the representation is genuinely organized as a variance-dominant linear function of the latents, and when the encoder behaves like a high-dimensional kernel/random-feature map where the latents are linearly readable but occupy only a small slice of the representation’s geometry. The goal is to provide practical diagnostics that distinguish a true “linear world model” from mere linear readout existence.","importance":"This is primarily an evaluation/interpretability methodology contribution that affects how pretraining researchers reason about representations. Many conclusions about objective functions (contrastive vs self-distillation vs multimodal alignment), robustness, and OOD behavior are supported by linear-probe results. The paper provides a more discriminative toolkit—reverse predictivity, spectral concentration, and OOD tests—that can prevent over-interpreting probe success as evidence of a clean linear factorization. For foundation models, the message generalizes: “linearly decodable” is not the same as “linearly organized,” and the difference matters for generalization and for mechanistic claims.","technical_approach":"$5a","experiments":"Key findings reported:\n- In simulation, random-feature encoders achieve high forward linear decoding, matching asymptotic random-features predictions, yet fail the asymmetric diagnostics (notably reverse predictivity and spectral concentration), consistent with a kernel-machine geometry. Does passing a test establish the intended claim? We develop four executable operations for changing physical descriptions: identifying redundancies (L1), coarse-graining dynamics (L2), transferring structure (L3), and inferring missing quantities from conservation imbalances (L4). We evaluate these operations and three interpretability settings through null acceptance (NAR), positive acceptance (PAR), and cons","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T15:13:57Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22445.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"很多“自动科学发现/可解释性”工作把某个可计算的验收测试（例如动力学降维的 closure、表征相似度的 CKA、SAE 特征的概念富集）当作“科学主张成立”的证据。本文要解决的问题是：通过这些描述性测试（descriptive test）到底能证明什么、不能证明什么？以及如何用可执行的“空对照/正对照 + 后果测试（consequence test）”把“通过测试”与“主张有意义”区分开。","importance":"对预训练本身不是方法贡献，但对“机制解释/表征分析/自动发现”这类会反哺预训练决策的研究很关键：它系统展示了常用验收指标会接受科学上空洞的候选（例如把所有状态映射为常数仍可零 closure error），也展示了“能拒绝空对照”仍不足以证明被接受对象真的支持预期解释。对做 LLM 训练动态分析、表征对齐、SAE 解释的人，直接影响的是：1）不要把单一指标当作证据闭环；2）需要设计与主张绑定的 consequence-level 验证（例如干预可迁移的功能效应），否则很容易把评测当成结论。","technical_approach":"核心做法是把“候选生成/概念变更”形式化为四类可执行操作，并为每类操作明确区分两种测试：\n1）L1 识别冗余（redundancy）：处理不可观测自由度/规范冗余与守恒对称的区分。描述性测试可能只看某种不变性或等价类结构；后果测试要求能区分‘规范冗余’与‘真实对称’在可观测量上的不同含义。\n2）L2 动力学粗粒化（coarse-graining / reduction）：描述性测试是 closure（降维变量能自洽预测未来）；后果测试是 observable-preservation（保留与科学主张相关的可观测动力学变化）。关键反例是常数映射 Q(x)=0：它在任何系统上都闭合，但不携带任何动力学信息。\n3）L3 结构迁移（transfer structure）：把一个系统中的结构（如守恒律/对称）迁移到另一个系统。描述性测试可看结构匹配；后果测试要求迁移后的结构在目标系统上产生可检验的物理后果，并用“破坏对称”的对照来检验。\n4）L4 从守恒不平衡推断缺失量（infer missing quantities from conservation imbalance）：描述性测试可能只看拟合/一致性；后果测试要求推断出的缺失量在噪声尺度之上、且能解释观测到的不平衡。\n\n在神经网络可解释性部分，作者把同一逻辑迁移到两类常见设置：\n- 表征相似度（CKA）：CKA 本身不绑定“对应关系”。随机旋转可保持 CKA 却破坏语义对应。作者用局部干预（intervention）来做 consequence test：如果映射是真的对应，应当能把功能性干预效应从一个表征空间迁移到另一个。\n- 稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征解释：概念富集（enrichment）能拒绝空特征，但被接受的特征未必在独立消融中表现出概念特异的因果支持。作者用 matched controls（匹配对照）与 positive controls（正对照）同时校准富集与干预测试的可靠性。\n\n方法论贡献用三个指标量化：NAR（null acceptance rate，空对照通过率）、PAR（positive acceptance rate，正对照通过率）、CAR（consequence agreement rate，描述性通过与后果支持的一致率）。这三个量把“测试是否会被空洞解钻空子”和“通过是否真支持主张”拆开了。","experiments":"关键结果集中在“描述性测试会被空洞候选通过”与“加入后果测试后才能形成可靠验收”。文中给了多组具体数字：\n- L2（动力学降维）在 dysts 公共混沌系统基准上：常数降维 Q(x)=0 在 129 个系统中有 124 个（约 96%）通过 closure；但 observable-preservation 测试在所有系统上都拒绝该空洞候选。\n- 在构造的 slow–fast 系统上：组合标准（closure + observable-preservation）接受 24/24 个已知真实降维，并拒绝 72 个空对照候选。\n- L1：区分 4 个守恒对称与 1 个 gauge redundancy（文中表述为“distinguish four conserved symmetries from a gauge redundancy”）。\n- L3：验证 6 个真实结构迁移并拒绝 6 个破坏对称的对照（“validate six genuine transfers while rejecting six broken-symmetry controls”）。\n- L4：恢复缺失电荷 1.192，对照 withheld 真值 1.200。\n- 神经可解释性：在 Pythia 语言模型与 ESM-2 蛋白语言模型上展示 CKA 的旋转不变性导致“高 CKA 但错误对应”，并用干预迁移来区分；在 SAE 上展示 enrichment 能拒绝空特征，但只有一部分被接受特征在独立消融中得到概念特异支持，同时用 matched/positive controls 说明两类测试各自的灵敏度与盲点。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 40–60 分钟）：\n- 先看 Fig.1（如果正文有总览图）：NAR/PAR/CAR 的定义与它们分别在回答什么问题。\n- 精看 L2 部分：常数映射 Q(x)=0 为什么能在 closure 下“完美通过”，以及 observable-preservation 如何把科学内容拉回来；重点找 dysts compute/不变性/对称性钻空子。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Many automated discovery and interpretability pipelines treat a convenient acceptance test (e.g., closure for dynamical reductions, CKA for representation similarity, concept enrichment for SAE features) as evidence that the intended scientific claim holds. The paper asks what such descriptive tests actually establish, demonstrates that they can accept scientifically empty candidates, and proposes a control- and consequence-driven evaluation protocol to separate “passes the test” from “supports the claim.”","importance":"Not a pretraining recipe paper, but highly relevant to research that feeds back into pretraining decisions via mechanistic analysis, representation alignment, and interpretability tooling. The main impact is methodological: (1) a single metric can be satisfied by degenerate solutions (so metric success is not evidence), and (2) even strong null rejection does not imply the accepted objects have independent causal support. Language model agents extend this setting through interaction: their actions determine what evidence enters context next. We call this regime Agentic In-Context Learning (Agentic ICL). Existing studies largely depend on externally supplied contexts or reward-bearing interaction histories, while work on general tool-using agents lacks a learning algorithm view. repres","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T07:28:42Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23051.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"该工作研究固定参数 Transformer 在闭环交互中能学会哪些“上下文内学习算法”。与传统 ICL 不同，Agentic ICL 中模型的行动会决定下一步进入上下文的证据，例如搜索结果、编译错误、实验观测或奖励反馈。","importance":"对 LLM 研究的价值在于把 agent 行为重新放回 learning algorithm view 下分析。传统 ICL 研究通常假设 prompt 已经给定；而工具使用、代码修复、网页检索和实验型 agent 都是闭环过程，模型不仅读取上下文，也通过行动选择未来上下文。这个问题会影响 agent 训练目标的设计：只看最终结果，可能得到与专家过程不同但结果可用的策略；加入过程监督，又可能限制探索或改变策略偏好。该工作提供了一个较干净的形式化接口，可用于分析 active learning、bandits 和 online RL 中 Transformer 的可表达性与训练偏差。","technical_approach":"作者把交互学习器拆成三个部分：充分状态、反馈更新规则和决策规则。环境由任务 M、kernel、有限动作空间和 horizon H 定义；模型在每一步看到历史 h_{t-1}，选择动作 a_t，环境返回记录 x_t，历史继续增长。关键点是反馈不必是标量 reward，可以是网页、诊断信息、测量值或 critique，因此 Agentic ICL 覆盖 ICRL，但不被 reward-history 设定限制。在理论上，作者给出构造性结果：有限 horizon 下的规则递归可以被 causal Transformer 近似实现，并对自主执行时的 return loss 给出界。训练目标部分区分 outcome supervision 和 process supervision，讨论什么条件下过程监督不会破坏 outcome-optimal 行为，并设计行为诊断来判断训练出的 Transformer 是否真正执行了目标交互算法。","experiments":"实验分两类。第一类是 supervised distillation：给定目标交互递归，测试 Transformer 能否在上下文中执行对应的更新和决策过程。第二类是没有 oracle action 的 RL 训练：比较 outcome reward 与 process reward 如何导致不同的动作选择，并通过可控的 reward intervention 观察策略变化。摘要和已给正文没有披露完整数值表、模型规模或具体任务设置，因此可确认的结论主要是定性结构：训练目标不仅影响最终成功率，也会改变模型在闭环中选择证据、更新状态和行动的方式。对 agent 研究而言，这比单纯报告 task score 更有诊断价值。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 30–45 分钟。先看 Section 3 的闭环定义和 state-update-decision 分解，确认它如何覆盖 active learning、bandits 和 online RL。再看 Section 4 的 Transformer through the model’s own actions. In this Agentic ICL setting, a query, tool call, code edit, or experiment determines what evidence enters the context next.","importance":"The contribution is a formal bridge between the learning-algorithm view of ICL and modern tool-using agents. Standard ICL analyses usually assume an externally supplied prompt. Agentic systems operate in a closed loop: they select actions, receive feedback, and then condition on the transcript they helped create. This distinction matters for training. Outcome-only objectives can favor policies that solve the task but do not follow a desired evidence-gathering procedure, while process rewards can preserve or distort the intended interactive algorithm depending on their design.","technical_approach":"The framework represents an interactive learner through a sufficient state, a feedback update, and a decision rule. An episode starts from an initial prompt, then at each step the frozen Transformer policy selects an action, the environment returns a record, and the transcript grows. The returned record need not be a scalar reward; it can be a search result, compiler diagnostic, measurement, critique, or reward. This makes in-context reinforcement learning a special case rather than the default assumption. On the expressivity side, the paper constructs causal Transformers that approximate regular finite-horizon recursions and provides a bound on the resulting autonomous return loss. On the training side, it analyzes when process supervision preserves outcome-optimal behavior and proposes behavioral diagnostics for learned interactive policies.","experiments":"The experimental program has two parts. Supervised distillation tests whether Transformers can learn to execute specified interactive recursions in context. RL experiments without oracle actions compare policies trained with outcome rewards versus process rewards, and use controlled reward interventions to examine how decisions change. The provided text does not include full quantitative tables, model sizes, or task-level numbers. The main confirmed takeaway is qualitative: training objectives shape not only final task performance but also the evidence-gathering and state-updating behavior that produces that performance.","reading_guide":"Skim in 30–45 minutes. specify","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T10:31:27Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23257.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 reasoning model 的 CoT controllability 到底有多强，以及现有评测是否低估了它。已有做法把“不要用某个词”“按某种格式思考”这类约束直接写在用户提示里，再看模型的思维链是否遵守。作者指出，这种 User-Instructed CoT 评测把“对最终回答的指令遵循”和“对思维链本身的控制”混在了一起：RLVR 和常见指令训练主要奖励最终答案，不会专门奖励模型去遵守关于 CoT 的用户约束，所以低分未必表示模型不会控制 CoT，更可能是模型在推理时把这类约束放在了较低优先级。论文要回答的核心问题不是‘模型能否控制 CoT’，而是‘当约束被放到 CoT 内部、成为模型自己的局部推理目标时，它是否其实能显著更好地控制 监控来发现不当行为的方案会更脆弱。对基座研究员来说，这篇论文的价值不在于提出了新的训练方法，而在于给出一个更贴近模型内部控制能力的评测视角，并暴露出 post-training 目标函数对 CoT 这一中间变量几乎没有显式约束的后果。量级上，它更像是一个评测与机制判断上的修正，而不是能力提升方法本身。","technical_approach":"方法很直接，但设计得比较干净。作者在 CoT-Control 基准上扩展出 Self-Instructed CoT Controllability：不再只把约束放在用户提示里，而是把约束写进 CoT 开头，作为后续推理必须遵守的 self-instruction。具体有两种主要诱导方式。第一种是 reasoning prefill：如果能访问 reasoning channel，就直接预填一段类似“我需要在推理中避免使用词 X / 按格式 Y 推理”的开头，让模型继续完成后续 CoT。第二种是 bootstrap：在用户提示中只要求模型先在 CoT 里复述一条自我提醒，再开始推理。这样即便拿不到显式 reasoning prefill，也能让模型先生成自我指令，再继续思考。论文还做了一个关键对照：把具体约束替换成泛化提醒，例如‘记得遵守用户对推理的要求’，如果这也能提升 controllability，就说明问题主要不在模型不会执行约束，而在它默认不把用户对 CoT 的要求当成高优先级目标。评测覆盖 QA 与 agent 两类任务，沿用 CoT-Control 的任务、约束和 grader，并加入 hard/soft 两类指标。hard metric 看是否完全满足约束；soft metric 则衡量部分遵守程度，更适合观察模型是否在朝约束方向调整。后半部分作者把 self-instruction 用到多轮 agent 场景，并构造监控规避攻击，检验这种控制能力是否会转化为实际风险。","experiments":"主要结果很明确。五个开源 reasoning model、九类约束、QA 与 Agent 两类基准上，self-instruction 都显著提高 CoT controllability。文中给出的代表性数字是：DeepSeek-R1 从 0.4% 提升到 22.2%，Qwen3-32B 从 2.5% 提升到 34.3%。如果看 soft metrics，提升幅度更大，说明很多失败样本并不是完全不会控，而是原始 user-instructed 设置下只做了很弱的局部调整。作者还发现，只在 CoT 开头加入一个泛化提醒，也能明显改善结果，这支持“模型在去优先化用户对 CoT 的要求”这一解释。对闭源模型，bootstrap 方式在 Claude 系列上也有效，说明方法不依赖开放 reasoning channel。多轮 agent 实验进一步表明，自我指令可以跨 turn 持续，控制效果还能继续增强。安全部分更值得注意：作者在一个较真实的 CoT monitoring 设置里设计 self-instructed attacks，能降低 monitor 的 catch rate，说明这种 controllability 不是纸面现象，而是足以影响监控有效性的操作性能力。论文摘要里没有完整列出所有 benchmark 的逐项数字，但从正文结构看，重点应放在 Figure 3、Table 2 以及 agent / monitor evasion 小节的分解结果上。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看引言和方法部分对 User-Instructed 与 Self-Instructed answer，那么模型学到的到底是‘会推理’，还是‘会生成足以支持答案的可编辑中间文本’。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether current evaluations are underestimating chain-of-thought controllability in reasoning models. Prior work measures controllability by placing constraints in the user prompt, such as forbidding a word or requiring a format, and then checking whether the model’s CoT obeys them. The authors argue that this setup conflates two different things: instruction following for the final answer versus control over the internal reasoning trace. Since RLVR and most instruction tuning reward the final answer rather than compliance of the CoT itself, poor performance in the user-instructed setting may reflect prioritization failure, not an inability to control CoT. The central question is therefore not simply whether models can control CoT, but whether they can do so much better once the constraint is embedded inside the CoT as a self-instruction.","importance":"Its value is mostly diagnostic rather than algorithmic. For pretraining and post-training research, the paper sharpens how to interpret low CoT controllability scores: they should not be taken as direct evidence that models lack internal control over reasoning traces. It also matters for safety. If models can shape their CoT much more effectively when prompted from within the reasoning stream, then CoT monitoring becomes a weaker oversight mechanism than user-prompt evaluations suggest. For foundation-model researchers, the contribution is a better measurement lens and a concrete demonstration that current training objectives leave CoT largely unconstrained as an intermediate variable. The impact is not a new training recipe, but a meaningful correction to how reasoning-model behavior is evaluated and how CoT-based oversight should be trusted.","technical_approach":"$5d","experiments":"$5e","reading_guide":"$5f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇在修正“CoT 不可控”的测量口径：把约束放进 CoT 后可控性大幅上升；建议只读设定与失败案例，顺手复现自家模型。","en":"It’s a protocol fix for “CoT is uncontrollable”: moving constraints into the CoT boosts compliance; read the setup and failure cases, then quickly replicate on your models."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Uriel Dolev","Idan Schwartz","Yoav Goldberg"],"notable_authors":["Yoav Goldberg"],"tags":["chain-of-thought","controllability","evaluation-protocol","mechanistic-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23257","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.23257","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.23801","title":"Beyond SAEs: Consistent Concept Discovery with Sparse Coding","abstract":"Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) explain foundation model representations by decomposing activations into sparse combinations of learned dictionary atoms. However, the learned dictionaries can vary across training runs, limiting the reliability and reproducibility of SAE-based analyses. We propose Consistent Sparse Dictionary Learning (ConSDL), which replaces the standard linear encoder with per-sample sparse coding. This is motivated by our synthetic analysis showing that coefficients from a single l","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T17:25:11Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23801.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"稀疏自编码器（SAE）在不同随机种子下训练出的字典存在显著差异，导致基于 SAE 的模型内部表示分析缺乏可靠性和可重复性。","importance":"提出了一种提升 SAE 字典学习一致性的新方法，对提高模型可解释性研究的可靠性有直接帮助，属于机制分析/可解释性方向的基础性改进。","technical_approach":"提出 Consistent Sparse Dictionary Learning (ConSDL)。核心是将 SAE 中标准的线性编码器替换为逐样本的稀疏编码（per-sample sparse coding）。具体而言，在每次更新字典前，使用 FISTA 算法对每个输入样本独立求解稀疏系数；为了缓解 L1 正则化带来的系数收缩问题，引入了无惩罚的系数重拟合（coefficient refitting）；同时加入共激活相似度正则化（co-active similarity regularizer）以减少字典冗余。","experiments":"在 16 个合成数据集上，ConSDL 实现了近乎完美的字典和系数恢复，即使在字典更大、原子选择概率极度不平衡等现有方法失效的场景下依然表现稳健。在视觉和语言基础模型的真实激活数据上，ConSDL 也大幅提升了跨随机种子的字典和系数一致性。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读 Method 部分关于如何用 FISTA 替代线性编码器以及系数重拟合的细节。预计阅读时间 15 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) learn substantially different dictionaries across random seeds, limiting the reliability and reproducibility of SAE-based analyses of model internal representations.","importance":"Introduces a novel method to improve the consistency of SAE dictionary learning, directly enhancing the reliability of interpretability research. This is a foundational improvement in the mechanism analysis/interpretability domain.","technical_approach":"Proposes Consistent Sparse Dictionary Learning (ConSDL). The core idea is to replace the standard linear encoder in SAEs with per-sample sparse coding. Specifically, before each dictionary update, it uses the FISTA algorithm to independently solve for sparse coefficients for each input sample. To mitigate the coefficient shrinkage caused by L1 regularization, it introduces penalty-free coefficient refitting. It also incorporates a co-active similarity regularizer to reduce dictionary redundancy.","experiments":"Across 16 synthetic settings, ConSDL achieves near-perfect dictionary and coefficient recovery, remaining robust even with larger dictionaries and highly imbalanced atom-selection probabilities where existing methods fail. On real activations from vision and language foundation models, ConSDL substantially improves cross-seed dictionary and coefficient consistency.","reading_guide":"Focus on the Method section detailing the replacement of the linear encoder with FISTA and the coefficient refitting process. Estimated reading time: 15 minutes.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"SAE 相关机制结论都受这个问题影响；建议看 synthetic 设定和 cross-seed 指标，别只看可视化案例。","en":"This affects many SAE-based mechanistic claims; read the synthetic setup and cross-seed metrics rather than the visualization examples."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jinho Choi","Hyesu Lim","Jaegul Choo","Steffen Schneider"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","sparse-autoencoders","sparse-coding","concept-discovery","reproducibility"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23801","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.23801","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.22717","title":"ARBOR: Locating Reasoning Failures in Long Chains of Thought through Reasoning State Tracking","abstract":"Large reasoning models solve complex problems through long chains of thought, in which prior work has documented failure modes such as overthinking, underthinking, and unsupported deductions. Existing methods detect these failures from local signals: textual similarity to nearby steps, switch tokens, or an LLM's judgment of a step's correctness. However, what a step contributes to the solution depends on the state that earlier steps have established, so identifying failures requires a global vie","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T22:39:54Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22717.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“某一步看起来像错了”，而是“这一步相对于此前已经建立的推理状态，到底错在什么关系上”。长链式推理里，重复、过早切换思路、无依据推出结论，往往都不能靠局部文本信号判断，因为同一句话在不同上下文里可能分别是必要推进、合理回溯，或纯粹空转。ARBOR把推理过程显式表示成一棵 reasoning-state tree：节点是已建立的中间结果，边记录当前步骤依附于哪个已有状态，失败则被定义为‘当前步骤与某个先前状态之间的关系错误’。它要解决的核心问题，是给长 CoT 中的失败做可归因、可重放、可细分的结构化定位，而不是继续依赖 step-local heuristic 或单步 judge。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在于提升预训练 loss，而在于把“过程监督”从文本表面推进到状态层。现在很多 reasoning 训练和评测仍把中间步骤当自然语言片段处理，导致 reward model、process supervision、trajectory filtering 很难区分真正有用的探索、合理回退和无效重复。ARBOR提供了一种更接近程序执行轨迹的标注框架：同一条轨迹可以被解析成确定性的状态演化，再把失败细分成与特定历史节点相关的子类型。这样的表示对后续几类问题都有启发：一是构造更干净的过程监督数据；二是分析长推理中的 token 浪费与正确率损失分别来自哪里；三是为 test-time intervention 提供更明确的触发条件。量级上，它更像一个高质量分析与标注框架，而不是新的训练范式，但如果要做 reasoning pretrain / mid-train / PRM 数据构建，这类工作是有实际接口价值的。","technical_approach":"方法分三段。第一段是 parser。作者先为每个任务定义 puzzle schema，包括实体、谓词、允许的操作、初始状态、目标等，再让 Qwen3.8-27B 把每个自然语言步骤解析成一个 operation 加 payload。operation 一共七类：Derive、Assume、Verify、Prune、Confirm、Restate、Meta。payload 是去表述差异后的规范化内容，比如事实、数值、动作、子目标、假设或答案。这样“Bob lives in the fifth house”和“house 5’s name is Bob”会落到同一个原子表示上。第二段是 deterministic engine。它不再调用模型，而是把 parser 输出按顺序回放到当前树状态上，解析该步骤应当附着到哪个节点，并执行相应 mutation。因为这一步是确定性的，所以同样的 parse 会得到同样的树和同样的 failure label。第三段是 failure attribution。作者不把 failure 定义成一句话本身的属性，而是定义成 predicate(step, resolved node, prior tree)。这样三大失败模式——repetition、underthinking、unsupported deduction——都能进一步拆成若干子类型，并且每个标签都能指出它是相对于哪个先前状态成立的。方法上的关键区别有两点：第一，它把“推理内容”与“推理操作”分开，先做 wording-invariant canonicalization，再做状态更新；第二，它把 failure 从文本分类问题改成状态机上的关系判定问题，因此能区分局部文本相似但功能不同的步骤。文中还强调，只有 parser 用到 LLM，后续 replay 和 labeling 都是可复现的，这一点对构建大规模过程数据很重要。","experiments":"实验覆盖三类结构差异较大的任务：ZebraLogic 约束满足、24 Game 算术组合搜索、PlanBench 带前置条件的顺序规划。parser 在人工裁决参考标签上的准确率在每个任务都达到 0.89 以上，这是整套框架可用的前提。核心结果有三类。第一，ARBOR 对失败的识别明显优于局部信号。作者比较了重复检测的 n-gram / embedding 相似度、切换词启发式、step correctness judge、以及若干 block-level label；结论是这些局部方法只能找回 ARBOR 标出的很小一部分失败步骤，而且误报里相当多是正常推理。更重要的是，ARBOR 的分 subtype 计数比把所有局部信号合起来，更能区分正确轨迹和错误轨迹。第二，不同 failure subtype 的代价不同。unsupported deduction 更直接伤害正确率，因为模型后续验证很少能把这种“状态并不支持的推出”纠正掉；underthinking 主要浪费搜索预算，常见情况是模型先放弃一条仍可成功的分支，后来又绕路回来或从别的路径到达答案，因此更多损失在 token 和时间，而不是最终正确率；repetition 对答案伤害最小，额外 token 成本也相对有限，通常只是把很早前提出过的结果重新建立一遍。第三，failure precursor 主要存在于树状态而不是单步标签里。作者分析了每种 subtype 发生前的 at-risk steps，发现决定因素往往是当前 branch 的内容、当前节点是否已被验证、以及最近一段操作序列。仅靠 operation label 只能恢复最后一种，而前两种恰好对应那些会影响 correctness 或浪费可行搜索工作的失败。摘要里没有给出完整表格数字，但文中主张这些结论在三类任务上都稳定成立，且 subtype 粒度比粗粒度 failure mode 更有解释力。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读但要抓住方法定义。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1/2/3，理解作者为什么坚持“failure 必须相对于 reasoning state 定义”，这比具体实现更重要。然后看 Method 里三件事：七类 operation 的定义、schema-grounded payload 的 canonicalization、以及 deterministic engine 如何把 step resolve 到树上。Table 1 和 Table 2 要仔细看，它们决定了 failure taxonomy 是否真的有信息量。实验部分建议重点看三处：一是 parser 可靠性，确认上游噪声是否可接受；二是 ARBOR 与 local signals 的对比，判断它到底是在发现新失败，还是只是换一种标注方式；三是 cost/precursor 分析，这部分最接近后续训练用途。若只想快速判断价值，30 到 40 分钟足够；若想把它用于 process analysis: failures should not be judged from a step in isolation, but from its relation to the reasoning state established so far. In long CoT trajectories, repetition, premature branch switching, and unsupported deductions are often indistinguishable from local text alone. ARBOR formalizes the trajectory as a reasoning-state tree, where nodes are intermediate results and each step is resolved against prior states. A failure is then defined as an incorrect relation between the current step and a specific earlier state, rather than a property of the step text itself.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is indirect but real. This is not a pretraining recipe, yet it is highly relevant to process supervision, reasoning-data curation, and trajectory-level diagnostics. Many current pipelines still treat intermediate reasoning as plain text, which makes it hard to separate useful exploration from wasted search or unsupported inference. ARBOR offers a more execution-like representation: trajectories become deterministic state transitions with subtype-level failure attribution. That matters for building cleaner process-reward data, measuring where token budget is lost in long reasoning, and designing test-time interventions that trigger on state conditions rather than surface cues. g","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T10:09:32Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23242.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Transformer 自注意力层里，某个很小的表征差分（perturbation / representation difference）在穿过一层后会收缩还是放大，取决于哪些机制？更具体地：当“仅值向量被传输（value transport）”会让差分收缩时，注意力权重的变化（attention routing / gating）是否能把它反转成放大；这种“反转”在什么条件下发生、发生在哪些方向上、强度多大、以及如何在真实预训练模型里可预测地找到这些方向。","importance":"这篇工作更偏机制与训练后分析，但对预训练研究有两类直接启发：\n1) 训练稳定性/梯度与敏感性：它把“注意力导致的放大”拆成可分析的子机制，并给出谱条件，能帮助理解为什么某些方向上 Jacobian 增益很大、为什么某些扰动会被层层放大（与 loss spike、对抗脆弱性、编辑外溢等现象相关）。\n2) 长上下文与信息保真：如果 gating 能在特定几何条件下抵消 value transport 的收缩，那么“信息是否被保留/被抹平”不仅是平均意义上的表示坍塌问题，还与注意力路由对差分的响应方向有关；这对理解长程依赖在深层网络中如何存活有参考价值。\n贡献量级属于“把一个常被笼统归因于 attention 的现象，拆成可验证的机制 + 可操作的判别条件”，不是直接给出新的预训练 recipe，但能为后续做训练动态/鲁棒性/编辑的研究提供更精确的语言与工具。","technical_approach":"核心方法是一个“Mixture-Gated Theory”的分解与线性化分析框架，把单头/多头自注意力输出对输入表征的微小扰动响应拆成三部分：\n- value transport：在注意力权重固定时，仅由于 V（被聚合的信息）变化导致的输出变化；直觉上更像“把差分搬运/平均”，常带来收缩。\n- query gating：扰动通过 Q 改变注意力分配（softmax 权重），从而改变输出；这是“路由响应”。\n- key gating：扰动通过 K 改变注意力分配。\n论文强调一个关键几何约束：即便注意力权重改变了，如果被注意到的 token 表征在某个子空间里几乎相同，那么在该子空间里挪动权重对输出几乎没影响；因此 gating 的有效方向受“被 attend 的表示集合的几何形状/张成子空间”限制。\n在此基础上提出 gating-induced regime switching：存在一些方向，value transport 单独作用会让差分收缩，但加入 gating 后整体变成放大。论文给出谱条件来判定是否可能发生 switching，并刻画：\n- 哪些方向会发生反转（switching directions）；\n- “最大子空间”：在该子空间内任意非零方向都发生 switching 的条件；\n- 两个强度度量：\n  1) relative switching strength：gating 相对 value transport 收缩的对抗程度（更像归一化指标）；\n  2) absolute reversal severity：即使 value transport 强力压制，gating 仍能把它放大的“绝对严重性”。\n此外还讨论了当相关算子需要从样本估计时（例如用有限扰动/有限 batch 估计局部线性算子），switching 方向的可靠性与误差敏感性。","experiments":"实验主线是“用预训练 Transformer 的真实隐藏状态做小扰动，验证理论对放大/收缩方向的预测”。从给出的全文信息可以确定的结论包括：\n- 观测到的现象：高 coverage 的随机搜索往往找不到“transport 强抑制但 full attention 放大”的方向；而基于谱分析/理论条件可以更直接定位这些方向，尤其在需要检查很多方向时更省样本。\n- 一个重要经验结论：relative switching strength 高，并不等价于 gating 能克服强 transport 抑制；因此只看相对指标会误判“真正危险/真正能放大”的方向，absolute reversal severity 更能区分“容易反转但本来就没怎么被压制”与“在强压制下仍能反转”。\n- 论文还对“估计误差下 switching The paper asks a specific causal question: can attention routing (changes in attention weights) turn a difference that would contract under fixed-weight value transport into one that amplifies? It then characterizes when such “gating-induced regime switching” is possible, which directions exhibit it, how strong it can be, and how to identify those directions in pretrained models.","importance":"This is primarily a mechanistic/theoretical analysis rather than a new pretraining recipe, but it matters for foundation-model pretraining in two practical ways:\n1) Sensitivity, Jacobians, and training stability: by decomposing attention’s local response into interpretable components and giving spectral conditions, it sharpens our understanding of why certain perturbation directions get amplified (relevant to loss spikes, adversarial fragility, and edit spillover).\n2) Information preservation across depth/long context: it suggests that “representation collapse vs preservation” is not only an average phenomenon; We introduce the concept of mechanistic uncertainty, which characterizes uncertainty through the stability of the internal mechanisms supporting a model's response. We propose the causal robustness margin, defined as the minimum internal intervention required to deviate the model's output, and operationalize it","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T20:46:06Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22636.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题是：LLM 的不确定性不只体现在输出概率、熵或自一致性上，也可能体现在内部计算机制是否稳定。作者把这种内部脆弱性定义为 mechanistic uncertainty，并用“让模型答案发生语义偏移所需的最小内部干预强度”来度量。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在机制分析和训练动态诊断。它不是直接改进预训练 recipe，而是提供了一种内部表征层面的不确定性信号。这个信号可以补充输出层 UQ 指标，用来判断模型回答是否建立在稳定的内部机制上。对基座模型训练来说，比较有价值的点是：知识注入后，原本答错但训练后答对的样本，其 causal robustness margin 会增大。这提示“学会某个知识”不只是输出正确，还可能表现为内部机制对扰动更稳。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 causal robustness margin。给定一个问题和模型的原始回答，作者在中间层 residual stream 上做受控干预，逐步增加干预强度 α，直到模型输出发生语义偏移。触发偏移所需的最小 α 记为 α*，α* 越小表示内部计算越脆弱，mechanistic uncertainty 越高。干预方向来自 Jacobian Lens。Jlens 用平均 Jacobian 把中间层激活线性传输到最终层，使中间表征可以被解释为“未来可能 verbalize 的 token/concept”。相比 logit lens 直接读中间层 logits，Jlens 更接近因果读出，因为它近似了后续层对该激活的线性响应。作者利用 Jlens vectors 对模型内部表示进行 concept steering，观察较小扰动是否足以改变最终回答。与常规 UQ 的区别在于，它不只看输出分布，而是问支撑该输出的内部路径是否抗干扰。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个开源模型和 5 个问答数据集。主要结果有三点。第一，错误回答通常有更小的 causal robustness margin，说明答错时内部机制更容易被较小干预改变。第二，该 margin 与输出侧不确定性指标互补，不只是复刻 entropy、likelihood 或 self-consistency。第三，知识注入实验显示，针对特定实体训练后，新变正确的回答对应的 margin 增大，说明成功学习会伴随更稳定的内部机制。提供的全文片段没有列出具体模型名称、数据集名称和数值提升，因此不应把结论解读为已经建立了可直接部署的校准指标。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中 mechanistic uncertainty 和 causal robustness margin 的定义，以及 Background 中 Jacobian Lens 如何把中间层激活映射到可 verbalize 的 token/concept。实验部分应优先看三类结果：margin 与正确性的相关性、与输出 UQ 指标的互补性、知识注入前后的 margin 变化。需要警惕两点：一是 Jlens 的线性近似是否足够稳定；二是“输出发生语义偏移”的判定方式会直接影响 α* 的可靠性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies uncertainty as an internal mechanistic property rather than only an output-level statistic. It defines mechanistic uncertainty as the fragility of the internal causal computation that supports a model response, and measures it by the minimum internal intervention needed to make the response semantically deviate.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is diagnostic. The method does not propose a new pretraining objective, but it offers an internal signal for whether a model’s answer is supported by a stable computation. This can be useful for analyzing training dynamics, knowledge acquisition, and failure modes. The knowledge-injection result is especially relevant: when training makes previously incorrect entity-specific answers correct, the causal robustness margin increases, suggesting that successful learning may correspond to more stable internal mechanisms, not merely changed surface outputs.","technical_approach":"The central quantity is the causal robustness margin. For a given prompt and baseline answer, the method applies controlled interventions to intermediate residual-stream activations and increases the intervention strength α until the generated answer changes semantically. The smallest such α is α*. Smaller α* means the response is easier to disrupt and therefore more mechanistically uncertain. The interventions are built using Jacobian Lens vectors. Jacobian Lens approximates the downstream effect of an intermediate activation through average Jacobians, allowing intermediate states to be read and steered in directions associated with verbalizable tokens or concepts. This differs from output-based uncertainty methods because it probes the robustness of the internal computation, rather than only inspecting likelihoods, entropy, verbalized confidence, or self-consistency.","experiments":"The experiments cover three open-source LLMs and five question-answering datasets. The main findings are: incorrect answers tend to have smaller causal robustness margins; the margin complements standard output-based uncertainty metrics; and in a controlled knowledge-injection setup, newly correct answers after training show larger margins. The provided text does not include exact model names, dataset names, or numerical effect sizes, so the result should be read as evidence for a useful mechanistic signal rather than a fully validated deployment-ready uncertainty estimator.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. 上：把任务需求拆成带权重的检查项，再由独立 verifier 对最终应用进行 build、run、inspect。终局奖励不是简单的 pass/fail，而是由确定性检查和视觉评估共同组成，并且受“应用能否成功执行和渲染”门控。为了让 RL 不只依赖稀疏终局奖励，作者又估计中间阶段的 requirement satisfaction，用阶段间分数变化构造 progress / regression 信号，给长轨迹做 credit assignment。另一个关键设计是支持跨 context window 训练。它结合 context compaction 和 message-tree sample construction：当对话历史过长时，对上下文做压缩，但项目真实状态仍保存在环境里；message tree 记录多次上下文重写后的历史依赖关系，使同一条长轨迹的不同阶段都能被用于训练，同时避免同一段已生成历史被重复计入 policy loss。这个设计比单纯截断上下文更贴近真实 agent 训练。","experiments":"主实验在 Webdev-Bench 和 Vision2Web 上。Webdev-Bench 是作者新建的 benchmark，强调在同一项目里组合多种开发需求，并要求保留指定既有行为。结果上，WebCoder-27B 在 Webdev-Bench 的 requirement satisfaction score 为 72.8，明显高于其基座 Qwen3.8-27B 的 64.8，也高于 Claude-Opus-4.8 的 65.1；并且在全部 40 个任务上都能交付可运行应用。Vision2Web 上，WebCoder-27B 的平均视觉与功能分数为 62.4，基座模型是 41.1，和 Claude-Opus-4.8 的 62.5 基本持平。若看方法有效性，最重要的不是单个 benchmark 数字，而是作者证明了三件事：1）统一的 artifact-grounded reward 能覆盖复现、生成、修改、修复等异构活动；2）中间进度信号确实改善了长程 credit assignment；3）context compaction 加 message-tree 训练能让 agent 在超长开发过程中继续学习，而不是因为窗口限制中断。若全文有消融，建议重点关注去掉 progress signal、去掉 verifier gating、去掉 context compaction 后的性能变化，因为这些最能说明方法是否真的在解决长程 agent 学习，而不是只靠更强 base model。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1/2，理解作者如何把前端开发抽象成“围绕共享 artifact 的能力组合”。然后看 Method 里三块：Webdev-World 环境接口、checklist-based verifier 与 reward 设计、跨窗口训练机制。实验部分优先看 Webdev-Bench 的任务构成和评分协议，再看与 base model、Claude 的对比。预计 25 分钟左右可以抓住主要信息。若只想判断是否值得深入，关键问题有两个：一是 reward 是否足够稳健，能否避免 verifier 被投机利用；二是这种 artifact-grounded RL 是否能迁移到更一般的软件工程或工具型 agent，而不只是前端页面开发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles long-horizon frontend development as a sequential agent-learning problem rather than a one-shot code generation task. A useful frontend agent must reproduce designs, generate new components, modify existing code, and repair regressions while the underlying project state keeps evolving. The hard part is not any single skill in isolation. It is how to convert heterogeneous execution feedback—build success, runtime behavior, visual fidelity, interaction correctness, and preservation of existing functionality—into a unified reward signal, and how to assign credit across long trajectories where later edits can undo earlier progress.","importance":"This is more of an agent post-training and environment-design paper than a core pretraining paper. Still, it is relevant in two ways. First, it presents a fairly mature artifact-grounded RL setup where learning is tied to an executable external object rather than text-only outputs. That is useful for code agents, tool-using agents, and multimodal action-oriented systems. Second, it addresses a practical issue that increasingly matters for model training: the task state can outlive the context window. The paper’s treatment of persistent environment state plus context compaction is a concrete example of how to train agents that operate over long horizons without forcing everything into the model’s token context.","technical_approach":"$68","experiments":"$69","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and the main method figure to understand the framing: frontend development is treated as capability composition over a persistent executable artifact. Indeed, harness and policy are mutually reinforcing and evolving only one component is suboptimal---a harness tuned for one checkpoint may not target the failure modes of an updated policy, while policy learning remains limited to the trajectories elicited by a fixed harness. In ligh","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T01:07:52Z","category_id":"coding_agent","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22791.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的核心不是单独把 agent policy 训得更强，也不是单独把 harness 调得更好，而是两者存在联动：固定 harness 会限制 policy 能暴露和学习到的行为，固定 policy 又会让 harness 搜索停留在过时的失败模式上。作者要解决的问题，是如何让 coding agent 在“外部交互脚手架”和“内部策略模型”两个层面递归共同演化，而且不依赖一个长期固定的外部 harness proposer。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 与 agentic training 的接口有现实意义。现在很多代码 agent 提升来自 prompt、工具调用、反馈格式、终止条件这些 harness 设计，而不是纯模型能力；同时，RL 或 trajectory learning 的上限又受制于 harness 能否把正确行为诱导出来。Self-HPO 把这两部分放进一个交替优化闭环，说明 agent 能力边界并不只在 base model，也在 interaction protocol。对基座研究员的启发主要有三点：第一，后训练中的环境设计本身是可学习对象；第二，policy 更新会改变之后最该优化的 harness 搜索空间，静态数据或静态 scaffold 容易过时；第三，pass@1 和 pass@k 同时提升，说明这种闭环不只是提高稳定性，也可能扩展可达行为集合。它更接近 agent post-training 方法论文，而不是通用预训练论文，因此放在 skim 是合适的。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Harness-Policy Optimization。设 coding agent 由 policy P 和 harness H 组成，任务执行轨迹由二者共同决定，并由可执行 verifier 给出二值反馈。作者把自我改进拆成两个交替步骤。H-step 固定当前 policy，用它分析失败轨迹并提出新的 harness 候选；候选可能修改提示词、上下文组织、工具使用流程、反馈暴露方式、终止规则等。然后在一个 search set 上评估这些候选，选出当前最优 harness。P-step 固定新 harness，在其下用强化学习更新 policy。更新后的 policy 再成为下一轮 harness proposer，同时上一轮训练中识别出的 hard tasks 被拿来构成下一轮 harness 搜索集，使 harness 搜索始终对准 policy 当前的薄弱点。作者实现了两个版本：Self-HPO 用 evolving policy 自己提出 harness；另一个参考版本用外部模型 Opus 4.8 作为固定 proposer。方法上的关键点不是“同时优化两部分”这句口号，而是两个具体设计：一是 harness proposer 由当前 policy 自举，避免外部 proposer 与被优化 policy 脱节；二是 harness search set 随 policy 演化，用训练中暴露出的 hard tasks 对齐新的 failure modes，而不是一直在固定开发集上搜索。论文还强调，优化后的 harness 和优化后的 policy 各自与 base counterpart 组合时仍保留部分收益，但联合收益超过简单相加，说明两者确实存在互补而非替代关系。","experiments":"基座 policy 是 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，初始 harness 是 mini-SWE-agent。评测覆盖三个 coding benchmark，文中给出的总体平均 resolve rate 从 57.8% 提升到 64.8%。在 SWE-Bench Verified 上，base 从 61.0% 提升到 69.5%；如果在同样的 HPO 框架里使用外部 proposer Opus 4.8，进一步到 71.6%。作者还报告这些收益能迁移到分布外设置，包括 SWE-Bench Multilingual 和 SWE-Bench Pro，说明优化后的 harness 没有只记住单一 benchmark 的表面模式。消融里最关键的一项是 search set 更新：用 policy learning 过程中识别出的 hard tasks 动态更新 harness 搜索集，优于反复使用固定搜索集。另一个重要观察是，Self-HPO 不只提升 pass@1，也提升 pass@k，这意味着它不仅让已有能力更稳定地被 harness 调出来，也让 agent 在多次尝试下能覆盖到更多原本不可达的成功轨迹。摘要没有展开 RL 细节和每轮增益曲线，但引言明确说平均 resolve rate 在每个优化步骤都持续上升，这支持“交替优化而非一次性联合调参”的设定。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓住 agent 分解：policy 决定能力上界，harness 决定这些能力如何被激发。Introduction 和 Figure 1/2 足够说明为什么单独做 harness optimization 或 policy optimization 都不够。方法部分重点看 H-step 和 P-step 的接口：失败轨迹如何被总结成 harness revision proposal，候选 harness 如何筛选，RL 在固定 harness 下如何训练，以及 hard-task search set 如何更新。实验部分建议优先看三类结果：一是 Self-HPO 相对 policy-only / harness-only 的增益；二是 optimized harness 与 optimized policy 的交叉组合实验，用来判断收益是否互补；三是 pass@k 与 OOD benchmark 结果，判断它是在做 exploit 还是确实扩展了行为覆盖。若只想了解思路，20 到 30 分钟足够；若想迁移到非 coding agent，需要细看 harness 变更空间的定义，因为这决定方法能否推广。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a coupled optimization problem in coding agents: policy and harness co-determine performance, so improving only one side is inherently limited. A fixed harness constrains which behaviors the policy can express and learn from, while a fixed policy makes harness search chase outdated failure modes. The core question is how to let both components evolve recursively, with the current policy itself driving harness improvement rather than relying on a permanently external proposer.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface and to agentic training more broadly. In coding agents, a large fraction of gains often comes from harness design—prompting, tool mediation, feedback exposure, and termination logic—rather than from the base model alone. Self-HPO turns this into an alternating optimization loop and makes a useful point for foundation-model researchers: the environment scaffold is itself an optimizable object, and its optimal form shifts as the policy changes. pattern；再用自定义压缩算法把低层轨迹解析成更大的 symbol blocks，寻找重复结构。随后 Gemini-based agent 根据压缩后的轨迹生成程序化转移规则，也就是 acceleration：用少量高层数学规则概括大量原始图灵机步骤。最后，系统把这些规则转成 Lean 形式化证明，验证 acceleration 与原始执行在指定状态表示下等价。这个设计的关键取舍是避免把 证明如何连接原始图灵机执行与 acceleration；失败案例是否来自轨迹不足、归纳错误还是形式化证明困难。对 LLM 基座研究员来说，不必深挖 Busy Beaver 的数学细节，重点是看 agent 如何把非结构化探索变成可验证产物。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether an AI system can go beyond solving human-specified math problems and instead discover mathematical structure from an open-ended computational frontier. The chosen testbed is the Busy Beaver domain, especially around roughly 1,000 unresolved BB(6) holdouts. The central challenge is to compress raw Turing-machine execution traces into higher-level transition rules, and to determine whether these rules expose Collatz-like dynamics or other Cryptids.","importance":"The paper is not a direct contribution to LLM pretraining, tokenizer design, or optimization. Its relevance is mainly for agentic scientific discovery and the interface between LLM-based conjecturing and formal verification. The useful idea is the closed loop: observe execution traces, infer compact rules, generate a formal statement, and verify equivalence in Lean. For researchers building foundation models, this is a concrete example of why open-ended discovery needs verifiable intermediate artifacts rather than free-form answer generation.","technical_approach":"The system treats each Turing machine as a grey box. It does not rely primarily on static analysis of the machine’s transition table. Instead, it simulates execution, records internal states and tape patterns, applies a custom compression procedure to identify symbolic blocks, and asks a Gemini-based agent to synthesize programmatic transition rules. These rules serve as accelerations: they summarize many low-level Turing-machine steps as compact arithmetic transitions. The final step is to translate the proposed acceleration into Lean and prove its equivalence to the original execution. The key design choice is behavior-driven structure discovery, which makes the approach less tied to one machine syntax. The risk is that trace-level induction can produce plausible but false rules unless formal verification is kept in the loop.","experiments":"The experimental setting focuses on about 1,000 BB(6) holdouts. What can be stated safely is that the paper positions the method as a way to accelerate execution and support Cryptid identification in the post-BB(5) Busy Beaver frontier.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20–30 minutes. These interfaces often change after deployment even when the underlying tool semantics remain fixed, creating a practical reliability problem for function-calling systems. We study this failure mode as interface drift and evaluate whether a lightweight, training-free repair layer can recover robustness without retraining the base model. Our method","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T11:49:37Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23342.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.SE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是 tool-calling agent 在部署后的一个很实际但常被忽略的问题：接口漂移。工具本身的语义没有变，但描述文本、参数名、枚举值、候选工具列表这些表层接口发生了变化，模型的 function calling 准确率会明显下降。作者关心的不是如何重新训练一个更强的 tool-use 模型，而是：在不改 base model、不做再训练的前提下，能否用一个轻量的推理时修复层，把这类由 interface drift 引起的错误尽量补回来，并且不伤害原本已经正确的样本。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接影响有限，因为它不是训练范式或数据方法论文；但对 post-train agent 系统和工具使用评测有现实启发。它把 function calling 的脆弱性明确归因到“文本接口依赖”，说明很多工具调用错误并不是语义能力不足，而是对接口表面形式过拟合。这个判断对训练数据构造、tool schema 设计、以及后训练阶段是否要加入 schema perturbation augmentation 都有参考价值。贡献量级上，它更像一篇扎实的系统修补论文：问题定义清楚，协议比较克制，结果也有用，但方法本身偏工程，不是新的建模范式。","technical_approach":"作者提出 SchemaShield-Lite，一个训练无关的 inference-time robustness layer，核心由三部分组成。第一步是 canonical rendering：把每个工具转成紧凑、规范化的 tool card，包含工具名、描述、必填字段和字段级类型信息，尽量弱化原始接口中冗余或过时的表述。第二步是 validation：模型先按漂移后的接口做一次普通 tool call，然后由验证器检查工具名、必填字段和参数结构，并输出结构化错误，而不是简单的 pass/fail。第三步是 repair：如果调用无效，就把任务上下文、canonical tool card、无效调用和验证错误一起喂回模型，请它做一次修复。非 oracle 协议下，repair 分两阶段。如果原始输出里已经给出了一个可见工具名，就直接针对该工具做受约束修复；如果连工具名都缺失，先在可见候选工具集合上做一次 candidate-set retry，把目标工具解析出来，再触发针对该工具的 constrained repair。作者还强调三个实现细节：repair prompt 要明确要求从对话中补全所有必填字段；缺失工具名时必须先做 candidate-set retry，否则后续受约束修复无法启动；repair 阶段需要更大的 token budget，文中把上限提到 512 以避免长对话上的修复输出被截断。方法上的关键判断是：真正起主要作用的不是 canonical card 本身，而是“结构化验证错误 + 面向已解析目标工具的受约束修复”这一组合。","experiments":"主实验集中在 Qwen3.5-9B，数据是 DICE-BENCH 的 300 条切片和 BFCL 的 200 条切片，并采用作者强调的 deployment-realistic non-oracle protocol。结果上，SchemaShield-Lite 在 DICE-300 上把准确率从 0.717 提升到 0.813，在 BFCL-200 上从 0.580 提升到 0.805。作者还特别检查了 originally-correct slice，没有观察到修复层把原本正确样本修坏的情况。为了排除“多试一次自然会更好”的解释，论文单独做了 naive retry baseline：DICE 上只有 0.727，而修复层是 0.813；BFCL 上 naive retry 为 0.610，而修复层达到 0.795。另一个关键发现是 missing tool names 是非 oracle 设置下的主要瓶颈。加入 candidate-set retry 后，修复后准确率在 DICE 上从 0.780 提升到 0.813，在 BFCL 上从 0.725 提升到 0.805，同时 unresolved repair targets 从 35 降到 15、从 34 降到 9。最终主实验中，DICE originally-correct slice 上只有 2/22 的 drift misses 仍然 validator-invisible，BFCL 上则是 0/45，说明大多数漂移导致的失败仍然能被验证器看见并进入修复流程。作者还在 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 上做了 BFCL 同协议复现，从 0.635 提升到 0.845，说明效果不只局限于单一模型规模，但最完整的非 oracle 证据仍然来自 Qwen 系列。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点不在理论，而在问题定义和实验协议是否站得住。先看 Problem Formulation，确认作者把 drift 限定为 semantics-preserving 的 description drift、schema drift、candidate-set drift，这决定了任务边界。然后看 Method 里的 repair pipeline，尤其是 validator 输出什么错误、candidate-set retry 在什么条件下触发、forced-tool repair 与 unconstrained repair 的差别。实验部分优先看主表和 6.2、6.5 这两个位置：前者回答‘是不是只是 retry 次数更多’，后者回答‘到底哪个组件在起作用’。预计 20 到 30 分钟能读完主线。更值得深挖的一点是，这类修复层虽然实用，但也暴露出当前 tool-calling 模型对接口表面形式的依赖很重；如果把这个现象前移到训练阶段，可能更合理的方向是做 schema augmentation、参数重命名鲁棒性训练，或者把工具语义表示从纯文本接口里部分解耦出来。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical failure mode in tool-augmented LLM agents: interface drift. The underlying tool semantics stay fixed, but the surface interface changes after deployment through renamed parameters, stale examples, revised descriptions, or expanded candidate tool lists. These benign changes can sharply reduce function-calling accuracy. The paper does not try to train a better tool-use model. Instead, it asks whether a lightweight inference-time repair layer can recover reliability under drift without retraining the base model and without damaging examples that were already correct.","importance":"Its direct relevance to pretraining is limited, since this is not a training-method or data paper. Existing memory gating policies are typically shared across users and therefore fail to account for user-specific patterns of future reuse. In this paper, we study memory gating at the fact level. We first propose a retrieval-based labeling strategy that labels each fact by whether it is later retrieved and incorporated into responses. We then formulat","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-16T17:34:45Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2610_23812.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是个性化 memory gating：在长期用户—LLM 交互里，系统从每轮对话抽取很多候选事实，但存储预算和上下文预算有限，必须决定哪些事实值得留下。已有方法多用对所有用户共享的启发式规则，或者从长上下文数据里学一个统一策略，难点在于不同用户对“什么值得记”差异很大，而且真正可靠的监督信号是延迟出现、且只对已存入的事实可见的。论文把这个问题明确建模为 one-sided delayed feedback 的 apple tasting 问题：未存入的事实永远看不到反事实收益，已存入的事实只有在未来被检索并被回答使用时，才暴露出效用信号。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接影响不大，它更接近 agent memory / online personalization；但对“如何从部署交互中提取可学习信号”这件事有方法论价值。它给出了一种比启发式标签更贴近真实使用价值的监督构造方式：用未来检索命中和回答采纳来定义 utility，再用 LLM judge 做校验。这种从下游使用轨迹反推训练标签的做法，对长期记忆、持续学习、用户建模，甚至部分 post-train 数据构造都有启发。贡献量级属于中等：问题定义清楚，反馈建模合理，算法是已有 bandit/UCB 思路与 warm start 的结合，亮点主要在任务形式化和标签管线，而不是提出全新的学习范式。","technical_approach":"方法分两部分。第一部分是标签构造。作者从多轮用户会话序列出发，先用现成 memory extractor（文中用 mem0）从每个 session 抽取 atomic facts，并在“无限预算、全部加入”的设定下构造候选记忆池。然后对每个候选事实，沿时间向后检查未来用户 turn：先判断该 turn 是否需要 memory，再执行检索；如果该事实在未来某次检索中被命中，并且其内容被后续回答真正采用，则把它视为有 utility。为了减少“检索命中但回答没真正用上”或“表面相似但语义无关”的噪声，作者加入 LLM-judge 验证环节。这样得到的标签不是基于静态类型规则，而是基于未来使用结果。\n\n第二部分是学习算法 Warm-Start UCB。离线阶段先利用历史用户数据学习一个共享的 utility pattern，相当于得到一个跨用户的先验或初始化模型；在线阶段再对新用户做 bandit 式自适应。由于反馈只在 admit 的事实上出现，而且要过一段未知延迟才知道是否有用，作者把它放进 one-sided delayed feedback 的线性决策框架里，用 UCB 在探索和利用之间做权衡：对高估值且高不确定性的事实更愿意存，从而逐步学到用户特有偏好。和纯离线监督不同，这里显式处理了两个现实约束：一是 partial feedback，二是 delayed feedback。和直接做 contextual bandit 不同，这里 warm start 让系统不必从零开始摸索，能先借助群体数据建立一个可用初始策略，再在个人层面偏移。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段看，实验主要围绕作者构建的 benchmark 展开，包含已有的 PerMemBench-S，以及额外合成的更长时程版本 PerMemBench-L，用来覆盖长期交互。评测维度包括不同 memory budget、不同 interaction length 下的 gating 效果，并与先前的 LLM-based gating 方法以及 admit-all 基线比较。论文声称 Warm-Start UCB 在这些设置下都能稳定优于既有方法和朴素全收策略，说明个性化在线适配确实能带来收益。\n\n更值得看的不是单一平均分，而是几类分析：第一，标签有效性分析，作者在附录做了 meta-evaluation，验证 retrieval-based utility label 是否真的对应“未来有用”；第二，共性与个性并存的统计，作者专门分析 utility pattern 在用户之间哪些部分可共享、哪些部分差异明显，这直接支撑 two-stage 设计；第三，理论部分给出 warm start + UCB 在利用离线数据并逐步逼近个体最优上的保证。由于你给的全文片段没有包含完整结果表和具体数值，我不能可靠编造提升幅度、样本量或 regret 常数；如果要精读，建议直接核对主表、附录 A.2/A.3 以及理论章节中的定理条件和实验设置是否一致。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读，不必逐行推公式。优先看三处。第一是 Introduction 和 Dataset Construction，重点看作者如何把“未来检索并被回答使用”定义成 utility，以及 LLM-judge 在标签去噪里扮演什么角色。这里决定了整个问题是否站得住。第二是方法章节里 Warm-Start UCB 的建模假设：上下文特征是什么、reward/feedback 如何定义、延迟如何进入更新、warm start 具体初始化了什么。第三是实验里的 ablation：离线预热去掉会怎样、只做共享策略或只做在线 UCB post-training，再补理论和附录统计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies personalized memory gating for long-horizon user–LLM interaction. A memory system extracts many candidate facts from each session, but storage and context budgets are limited, so it must decide what to keep. Existing systems mostly use user-agnostic heuristics or a single policy shared across users. The hard part is that utility is user-specific, only partially observed, and delayed: if a fact is not stored, its counterfactual value is never observed; if it is stored, its usefulness becomes visible only later, when it is retrieved and actually used in a response.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the pretrain↔deployment loop because it shows how to derive learning signals from real interaction traces rather than from static heuristics. That is a cleaner supervision source than hand-written memory rules. Scaling width does not remove projection error in low-rank optimization; under memory-constrained training, the rank/hidden ratio often becomes smaller, making stale projectors more costly. Streaming tracking directly reduces projection-capture error and optimizer-state reset error. These are geometric errors, not benchmark-specific artifacts, so they are less likely to disappear at 100x compute."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证低秩优化器的关键瓶颈是否来自 projector staleness，并判断 streaming k-PCA + state transport 的收益是否随尺度保持或扩大。","en":"Test whether projector staleness is the key bottleneck in low-rank optimizers and whether streaming k-PCA plus state transport maintains or increases its advantage with scale."},"data_sources":{"zh":"LLaMA-style dense 模型 150M、400M、1B；FineWeb-Edu/RefinedWeb + code/math 小比例混合；每个模型训练 20B–80B tokens，按 Iso-FLOPs ladder 控制。比较 full-rank AdamW、GaLore 周期 SVD、GaLore 高频 SVD、Ori-style streaming k-PCA，以及去掉 state transport 的 streaming 变体。","en":"LLaMA-style dense models at 150M, 400M, and 1B; FineWeb-Edu/RefinedWeb plus a small code/math mixture; train each for 20B–80B tokens under an Iso-FLOPs ladder.
